## Surprises & Discoveries

- An authenticated vocabulary counterexample disproves token-count preservation when reasoning is inserted into a later prompt: normal tokens accro + ubic re-tokenize to acc + rou + bic. This invalidates a proposed accounting shortcut, not the existing byte bound, and establishes neither actual sampled output nor current-case overflow. See [M602-SCHEMA-FOLLOWUP](#m602-schema-followup--retokenization-counterexample).

- Native schema enforcement with reasoning conditionally re-renders accumulated thinking into a second completion. Each completion receives the full prediction cap; adding format would not preserve the current combined output allowance. The known-byte tokenizer bound does not establish the missing worst-case decoded/re-tokenized reasoning bound. See [M602-SCHEMA-FEASIBILITY](#m602-schema-feasibility--native-schema-and-reasoning-accounting).

- The uncertainty request already contains exact per-case reference enums and an instruction avoiding forbidden stems. Local reasoning intentionally omits native format enforcement. Offline probes reproduce identical reminder diagnostics for affirmative and negated claims; this is the frozen lexical policy, not proof of the original wording.

- The completed observations show recurring loss of the required blocking human judgment: a generic marker, no/false, None, or a blank field. Groq image is rejected for blockingManualJudgment/prose-blank. The unchanged field schema admits strings that still fail application or semantic requirements; these outputs establish the behavior, not a causal proof that prompting alone will fix it.

- Reasoning Local image completed in77,669ms with valid structure and qualified observations, yet failed the unchanged semantic rubric. Local label subsequently failed its first version request before generation; no Ollama process/listener remained. Why the developer runtime exited is unknown. See [the retained result](#m602-resolve-result-01--reasoning-campaign-stopped-at-local-label).

- The clean Windows checkout converted the authenticated successor manifest to CRLF; [BUG-0002](../../bugs/bug-0002-successor-manifest-checkout-bytes.md) records its independently verified committed-byte recovery. Historical source/test hashes also differ, so fresh executable verification replaces reuse of earlier passing suites.
- M6-01 is Complete with 883 maintained tests and three current actual retrieval observations. G1 still retrieves unsuitable decorative-image guidance despite complete roles; the controlled generation exception does not repair that failure.
- The runtime uses `m302-instructions-v2`; the immutable M3-01 package uses different instruction bytes. Normal request construction substitutes the runtime instructions and reserializes input. Calling it unchanged would not execute the frozen definition.
- Existing adapter preparers can consume two well-formed messages, but bind runtime configuration and schema object identity. Read-only inspection found the parsed frozen schema deeply equal to the runtime schema, while serialized property ordering differs. Semantic equality is not byte equality; G must make the exact permitted mapping explicit.
- The production proposal validator requires a scored `RetrievalResult`, and persisted invocation validation rejects evaluation-only M3-01 prompt tuples. Forging a retrieval result or a production `run.json` is not a valid bridge. The M3-01 static verification code is a reusable reference, not an executable six-case runner.
- The outer generation stage, not transport alone, owns total deadline, single-use dispatch, duplicate detection and invocation accounting. A caller bypassing that stage must prove those boundaries without introducing general orchestration.
- Historical final Red fixtures passed the Groq harness instead of its `io` member and gave the real preparer an invalid request. Both defects were reproduced and subsequently repaired under the recorded owner exception. A missing-module failure alone did not establish those assertions were valid.

## Decision Log

- **2026-09-23 / Primary — Native schema with initial-only admission.** The owner selects option 2. The reviewed amendment explicitly trades complete advance two-pass fit assurance for bounded runtime failure, preserves reasoning and strict validation, and fixes the neutral reminder under new Local identities. Historical records and Groq remain unchanged; offline implementation grants no new live allowance. See [M602-NATIVE-01](#m602-native-01--owner-selected-native-schema-and-bounded-admission).

- **2026-09-22 / Primary — Bounded profile-judgment correction.** The owner selected the proposed implementation. Reuse existing profile text, schema enum specialization and candidate-validation boundaries; version the changed instructions/schema instead of changing historical meanings. Keep semantic support with the human reviewer. No model, sampling, reasoning-duration, corpus, fixture or hardware change is selected, and no new capacity benchmark is needed. See M602-JUDGMENT-01.

- **2026-09-22 / Primary — Preserve the prerequisite stop.** The image's semantic rejection permits distinct-case continuation under the frozen predicate; the label's missing prerequisite and cleanup flag do not. Stop without retry, restart, prompt change or further case dispatch. Runtime absence is not evidence of model-quality failure.

- **2026-09-21 / Primary — Preserve the completion stop.** Accept the recorded Local image response-validation failure and failed runtime observation without retry or fabricated assessment. Confirm current cleanup, preserve all earlier results and keep M6-02 In progress. The 986-test implementation result is not six-case completion.

- **2026-09-21 / Primary — Separate completion identity.** Accept the owner-authorized finite completion campaign contract after fresh R3 review. Preserve both stopped campaigns and unchanged model/inputs/controls; a first failure stops this campaign too. Canonical-byte setup is a prerequisite, not model-output repair.
- **2026-09-20 / Primary — Planning only.** Create one M6-02 plan and keep M6-03/M6-04 unselected; no model or account operation is needed to author it.
- **2026-09-20 / Primary — Freeze preservation.** Retain the original M3-01 bytes and accepted Groq admission amendment. A newer production prompt is not permission to revise fixed inputs.
- **2026-09-20 / Primary — Bounded mechanism selection remains G.** Compare existing direct procedures before custom code. The execution bridge is not selected by this plan; its identity, privacy and replay risks require the R3 contract below before implementation.
- **2026-09-20 / Primary — No success-driven retries.** Six distinct fixed cases are the entire evaluation set. Failed or unknown outcomes remain evidence; neither code-correction budgets nor human editing replenish actual-call allowances.
- **2026-09-20 / Primary — Test budget stop.** Reject the final Red handoff on reproduced fixture defects and preserve the exhausted chain. Do not open Green, invent attempt 4, replace the test owner to reset allowance, or invoke an implicit primary correction. Owner disposition is required by M602-A-RED-02.

## Purpose / Big Picture

Demonstrate one structured-generation execution for each of the three controlled profiles through Local Qwen and the same three through Groq. A reader can trace each original result to identical frozen evidence/guidance and evaluate structure, citations, groundedness, useful remediation, human judgment, prohibited claims and completion separately. This is a six-case portfolio observation, not a provider comparison or release qualification.

BHV-03/04 identify the same derived boundaries. The accepted evaluation exception narrows actual-retrieval eligibility only for these six controlled inputs.

### Immutable cases, controls and meaning

[evaluation/m301-generation-v1.json](../../../evaluation/m301-generation-v1.json) has SHA-256 `63770583a97e1d0517337474dadc706c1047d78e026e22c7a745ce7674ed9e6b`. All 13 referenced files, including the nine ignored files under `temp/m301-generation-freeze-v1/`, matched at entry. `package-lock.json` remains `38ce94019310a5efdba9bb5565abe6eb13f678fa18fcd9d87b1c5dc21b30748d`.

The six manifest entries are paired by profile, not six independently rewritten prompts:

| informative-image-alt | image-alt | Local image | Groq image |
| form-input-label | label | Local label | Groq label |
| text-contrast | color-contrast | Local contrast | Groq contrast |

Use the manifest's exact case/mode/input/instruction/schema hashes; the table labels are navigation, not new artifact identities. Its message assembly requires exactly two messages: exact frozen instruction text including final LF, then exact case input JSON text including final LF. Parse the exact frozen schema for the designated schema field; any checked equivalent runtime object must preserve the declared contract and disclose the actual wire representation. No runtime prompt substitution, reserialization of message text, additional history or case-specific instruction.

Every package has complete selected native facts and exactly three canonical role passages. Independently assembled guidance contains no fabricated score, vector, ranking or embedding provenance. Do not import M6-01 G1's real passages into this package, or portray a controlled success as improved retrieval. Preserve historical M2-04 abstentions and the current G1 semantic limitation separately.

Both modes preserve the manifest's 4096 output ceiling, temperature 0, top-p 1, one response, no streaming, and 120000-ms request deadline. The manifest owns all other explicit/omitted controls.

Before output inspection, bind the [accepted Groq amendment](../../requirements/evaluation-and-release/EVALUATION_AND_ACCEPTANCE.md#groq-admission-amendment-to-the-frozen-generation-definition) with the original manifest and `m304-groq-request-bytes-v1`: at most 65536 UTF-8 bytes for the complete measured-and-sent body. This is not hosted token fit or proof of complete input consumption. Local still requires complete context fit including schema/template/protocol overhead and output reserve; no truncation or widening.

On 2026-09-20, Groq's [model catalog](https://console.groq.com/docs/models) lists `openai/gpt-oss-20b`, and its [Structured Outputs documentation](https://console.groq.com/docs/structured-outputs) lists strict mode support. The [deprecation page](https://console.groq.com/docs/deprecations) was checked; no retirement of this exact model was found. These are dated public documentation observations, not account access or continued availability. Recheck at execution, including account-specific limits without printing secrets or sending a synthetic capability request. Provider documentation never overrides local runtime validation or no-retry rules.

## Scope and Non-Goals

In scope: freeze an honest execution binding for the existing six packages; implement only the demonstrated missing caller/validation boundary; qualify it offline; execute exactly the six authorized fixed cases; evaluate original outputs against the frozen rubric; preserve local content-safe evidence and close documentation.

Excluded: new model/provider/prompt/schema/corpus/gold, tuning or output repair, extra generation calls, warm-up/capability probes, retries, fallback, batching or concurrent cases, new embeddings/scans, capacity reruns, production eligibility bypasses, canonical aggregate format expansion, retained human decisions, UI redesign, general evaluation framework, scheduler, ledger subsystem, telemetry, statistical scores, multiple semantic evaluators or provider ranking. No model/runtime/dependency acquisition, credential modification, commit or push. A meaningful expansion requires owner authority before dependent work.

## Plan of Work

### G — Freeze the evaluation execution contract

After execution authorization, record current HEAD and dirty paths, relevant source/test/caller/configuration identity, frozen references, protected run/evidence inventories and current prerequisites. Never require the historical planning SHA or an uncommitted plan. Preserve unrelated work and use fresh lease baselines consistently.

Perform the bounded R3 decision work below before choosing the execution bridge. Compare a direct documented procedure using existing adapters and M3-01 validation precedent with the smallest reusable validation/execution extraction, if needed. Neither route may fake a RetrievalResult, silently substitute instructions, or present runtime configuration labels as the actual frozen wire prompt identity. Do not build custom tooling merely because no all-in-one runner exists.

The primary authors `M602-G-CONTRACT-01` after required synthesis/review. It must bind:

- exact six-entry identity, authorization, sequential order, explicit one-case invocation syntax, exclusive output paths and anti-replay checks;
- complete frozen input/provenance/canonical support checks and schema equivalence/representation; distinction between actual message identity and adapter runtime configuration;
- current Local digest/runtime/template/parser/effective context/offload and unchanged material controls; Groq endpoint/model/defaults, accepted byte policy and private credential boundary;
- common candidate validation without synthetic retrieval provenance; full deadline, single-use attempt capability, sticky duplicate handling, normalized failures and resource-terminal cleanup;
- local evidence shape, write-before-effect/uncertainty handling, original validated output identity, secret filtering and no raw envelope/hidden-reasoning retention;
- one semantic evaluator, frozen rubric, exact per-case outcome rules, and practical Local UI/OOM observation during each call;
- exact commands, allowed paths, negative test contract, cleanup and stop rules.

No production interface expansion or persistence-schema change is presumed. Keep evaluation records separate from production aggregates unless an already-authorized truthful representation is demonstrated. The exception creates no runtime flag, route or bypass. A schema/contract incompatibility that cannot be resolved within unchanged semantics returns for owner decision; do not amend M3-01 to unblock a caller.

Public documentation checks and owner-provided non-secret account-limit information are permitted prerequisites. Adapter-owned runtime metadata/credential checks occur only within the bound actual case, not a separate synthetic generation probe. Define request accounting separately for metadata and generation. Missing credentials or runtime may block B while unaffected A/C diagnostics continue.

### A — Qualify only the missing execution boundary

Use workflow ID `m602-six-fixed-generation-executions`, with one coherent `A-fixed-case-execution` slice unless G demonstrates separable responsibilities. Primary supplies the exact Milestone Assignment Packet v2 and responsibility contract; custom roles retain their configured model pins.

Use read-only `test_worker` behavior preflight. `EXISTING_AND_COVERED` reuses current evidence; `EXISTING_BUT_UNCOVERED` uses guarded `evidence` characterization without fabricated Red. Missing/regressed executable behavior uses test-worker Red → accepted test boundary → separate `code_worker` Green/optional behavior-preserving Refactor. Test-only helpers for existing-path characterization remain test-owned; a new substantive execution or validation mechanism is not disguised as a test fixture or non-TDD setup. The preflight classification and exact ownership resolve that distinction before writing.

Candidate paths are a purpose-named M6-02 test and bounded helper under `tests/`, plus only specifically justified existing generation validation/execution modules if extraction is necessary. This is not a broad lease. G freezes exact files, symbols and permitted structural refactor before dispatch. Documentation remains primary-owned; frozen files, historical results, credentials, Git metadata and unrelated source are forbidden.

Responsibility placement: frozen package authentication, one-case execution and output validation are distinct current concerns; reuse existing canonical support, adapter fit, HTTP transport and validation logic. Avoid copied provider implementations or a second diverging proposal policy. No production module imports test helpers or local frozen files. A test-only caller must not become a generic framework; production extraction is permitted only when current duplication or the accepted contract demonstrates its necessity.

Each worker write turn needs a primary-opened lease and fresh packet/digest, terminal compliant close, actual diff inspection, strict/focused evidence and cohesion disposition. One lease per worktree; primary documentation/guard maintenance occurs between leases. Accepted tests remain unchanged during Green. Workers use Git read-only.

Offline qualification covers at least: frozen-byte/canonical/profile mismatch; changed instruction or message newline; schema drift; missing/conflicting roles; Local full-fit and Groq boundary/body correspondence; immutable mode/exact endpoint; zero calls on pre-call failure; duplicate/reentrant dispatch; timeout/abort/ambiguous result; malformed, incomplete or prohibited candidate; invented references; secret echo; occupied output path; result-publication/cleanup failure; re-entry after uncertain effect. Use existing controlled transport doubles, never real calls for negative tests. Default tests must not require ignored inputs or credentials, and an opt-in real command must fail closed rather than skip missing prerequisites.

Fresh S3 `critical_reviewer` acceptance is required before any actual-case use: the named triggers are credentials/egress, frozen identity, single-attempt accounting and cleanup/recovery. Initial and one ordinary correction write per unchanged role/phase chain; attempt 3 only under the workflow's explicit progress/new-evidence/changed-action/expected-benefit conditions. One review-correction loop uses remaining budgets. Stop repeated decisive failure without new evidence, two no-diff handoffs, changed binding fields or exhaustion; primary triages without resetting counters. Consult the [bug index](../../bugs/README.md) for substantive defects.

### B — Execute six fixed cases and apply the frozen rubric

Only after owner execution authorization, G acceptance, A critical acceptance, exact prerequisite confirmation and zero unresolved required gaps may B begin. Freeze the order before output inspection: the three Local profiles sequentially, then the same three Groq profiles sequentially. Each entry is an explicit single-case operation, never a loop/batch command that automatically continues after failure.

One future execution authorization for this plan covers at most one entry per fixed case and one generation dispatch per entry, six maximum. Track six-entry status, whether dispatch actually occurred, response/validation outcome and remaining allowance in this plan and the bounded local result records. Do not claim a pre-call failure is a generation execution. An entered operation with no call is still not automatically replayable; ambiguous dispatch consumes its allowance pending explicit owner disposition. No seventh call, warm-up, retry or replacement is authorized.

Run on the reference PC with the current application available for a small existing-UI responsiveness observation during each Local call, without Analyze, retrieval or another Generate. G binds the exact benign interaction, time correlation, readiness/stop procedure and readily observable memory/OOM evidence; no new UI, benchmark tool or numeric performance gate. The prior capacity screen cannot prove these three calls remained usable. If an in-call observation is missed, report the missing proof and do not repeat generation to obtain it. No new embedding work is needed to populate a synthetic full-stack claim.

Before each operation, confirm its unconsumed identity, unchanged frozen/wire configuration, protected paths, private output destination and current necessary prerequisites. Use the fixed adapter; credential material stays inside Groq's existing secret-handling path and is neither printed nor inspected in chat. Record metadata/request counts and whether invocation started honestly. Preserve original evidence on any failure.

Stop subsequent actual generation at the first invalid/failed/unknown case, Local OOM/unusable interface, model/configuration drift, suspected secret exposure, identity discrepancy or cleanup uncertainty. Preserve the failure and complete unaffected offline/documentation work. ADR-0003/0014 and the frozen failure interpretation require owner disposition where the fixed configuration fails; do not switch model, provider, cap or prompt. Corrections and any new evidence campaign follow REQ-EVAL-005/007 and cannot silently replenish this six-case set.

One semantic evaluator applies the frozen M3-01 rubric to original outputs. Primary may fill that role; independent reviewers audit method, binding and evidence rather than becoming extra semantic scorers. For each profile keep structural validity, reference/citation validity and actual support, groundedness, remediation usefulness, confidence/uncertainty, blocking judgment/reminder, prohibited claims and provider completion separate, using the frozen pass/fail/not-run vocabulary. Mechanical pass does not prove semantic support. A human edit cannot retroactively pass the original answer and no actual review decision is created by scoring it.

Retain separate Local and Groq sections here, with one row per case, relevant identity, outcome, separate dimensions, original validated-output reference/hash and limitations. Detailed validated proposals stay in ignored local evidence; tracked text contains no prompts, raw responses, screenshots or private payloads. Do not retain raw provider envelopes, invalid secret-bearing content or hidden reasoning. G defines safe bounded failure evidence. Shared deterministic outcomes are linked to M6-01, not rerun as additional fixed cases or claimed as actual retrieval for controlled packages.

### C — Verify integration and close documentation

After the last accepted executable change, run the current maintained full suite (46 files/883 tests at entry, plus accepted additions), independent strict TypeScript and current build. Run focused checks at earlier boundaries; reuse a valid full run under the complete evidence-identity rule rather than repeating it at handoff. No real-call replay is part of regression.

A different fresh `critical_reviewer` audits the integrated six-case count, frozen/actual wire binding, validated and semantically interpreted original results, Local usability/OOM evidence, secret filtering, preservation, cleanup and documentation. It may inspect retained evidence read-only and reproduce offline risk-critical checks; it cannot make a seventh request.

Task completion requires its Verification, not merely six terminal records. Any failed case or unmet Must keeps completion unmet; report exact completed/failed/not-run entries and obtain necessary owner disposition without relabeling failure as success. Mark Complete and archive only after all required evidence, final review and the [documentation closure gate](../../README.md#task-closure-documentation-gate) pass. Reconcile roadmap, instructions, plan/progress navigation and materially affected summaries; M6-03/M6-04 remain unselected.

## Decision Review Contract

**Owner/artifact:** M6-02, `M602-G-CONTRACT-01` inside this plan. **Tier:** R3 for the execution-bridge decision: frozen versus runtime identity, private egress/credential handling, one-call capability and uncertain-result recovery. The original planning checkpoint mapped facts only. Subsequent owner-authorized research, synthesis, primary authorship and both fresh research reviews are complete; G is accepted below. A/B implementation and actual evidence remain required.

**Question:** What is the smallest truthful, testable way to execute the six unchanged packages through the accepted providers while retaining the common validation and failure contract without fake retrieval or production provenance?

**Unranked candidates for G:** direct documented assembly around existing adapter factories plus reusable M3-01 static-validation logic; or minimal extraction/reuse of production validation/execution primitives with a narrowly scoped evaluation caller. Eliminate any candidate requiring changed frozen messages, fabricated RetrievalResult, false invocation identity, skipped validation, duplicated provider stack or general workflow machinery. A successor manifest/architecture change is an owner decision, not a third implicit implementation route.

**Criteria:** exact accepted semantics/bytes, authentic provider path, full admission/validation and privacy, honest minimal records, no-replay/cleanup truth, maintained testability, responsibility cohesion and least code/procedure. Hard gates outrank convenience. No provider ranking, cost study or model research.

**Routing/budget:** At execution, primary supplies a Research Assignment Capsule v1 with exact sources and this invariant packet. One bounded non-ranking discovery pass may close remaining source questions. Assign one `critical_researcher` to the coupled identity/validation/attempt-boundary question; split only a demonstrably independent unresolved critical dimension. Each assigned researcher has one initial report plus one bounded follow-up. Mandatory `decision_analyst` returns DRAFT READY, RETURN FOR RESEARCH or OWNER DIRECTION. Require a fresh `critical_research_reviewer` pre-draft checkpoint, primary-authored contract, and a different final critical research reviewer. No drafting agent is needed. Apply canonical research correction/stopping limits; never treat role capacity as a staffing target. Required implementation reviews remain separate S3 acceptance of executable code.

| Invariant | Trigger / expected result | Proof and reviewer |
| --- | --- | --- |
| G1 completeness and authority | Every six-case contract/command/retention field resolved; unknown model/account facts stop affected execution | Analyst and both contract reviewers inspected entire artifact; G accepted, A/B proof pending |
| G2 frozen identity | Corrupt hash/newline/order or wrong case rejected before effect; exact messages and schema meaning preserved; runtime labels never misstate actual prompt | Critical researcher; offline vectors and contract reviewers |
| G3 truthful support | Wrong/missing/conflicting canonical guidance rejected or abstains without call; no scored RetrievalResult invented; same candidate validation rules | Researcher plus A critical validation review |
| G4 privacy and fit | Fixed destination/model, only minimized selected content, credential transport-only, no truncation; complete Local fit and measured/sent Groq bytes | Researcher plus negative/native-double evidence |
| G5 one operation and recovery | Occupied/entered/unknown case cannot replay; duplicate/abort/deadline cannot silently dispatch again or erase attempted provenance | Researcher plus A critical failure-path reproduction |
| G6 evidence honesty | Failed output remains failed, original preserved, one semantic evaluator, Local usability observed; no six-case completion with unmet mandatory evidence | Analyst, primary evaluator and different final implementation reviewer |

Decide the interface, provenance representation, validation equivalence, operation-consumption point, failure policy and retention boundary now in G. A/B prove those defined semantics; they cannot decide them after seeing output. Re-run the full invariant packet after a material revision. A required scope/authority change or exhausted budget returns to the owner. No R3 verdict grants provider calls without execution authorization and A acceptance.

## Concrete Steps

The execution commands below preserve the procedures used by the stopped campaigns. Start with [Current state](#current-state): no original, successor or completion execution is currently permitted after their recorded stops. The [evidence inspection guide](../../../README.md#inspecting-m6-02-generation-evidence) distinguishes read-only inspection from replay and explains revision-bound successor readback.

Working directory: repository root, PowerShell 7. Load only the definitions under [Development command preparation](../../../README.md#development-command-preparation), which supply `Invoke-M105Command`, pinned Node and exact environment restoration. Do not replay adjacent npm-ci, archived generation, acquisition or cleanup commands.

### M602-CMD-ENTRY — Read-only planning/re-entry

```powershell
git status --short
git rev-parse HEAD
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath 'logs/agent-flow-leases/v2/active.json') {
  throw 'Reconcile active lease before M6-02 maintenance or execution'
}
Invoke-M105Command {
  if ((& $m105Node --version) -ne 'v24.20.0' -or $LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
    throw 'Pinned Node runtime unavailable'
  }
  & $m105Node node_modules/typescript/bin/tsc --project tsconfig.json
  if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Independent strict TypeScript failed' }
  & $m105Node --test --test-timeout=120000 tests/generation-contract.test.ts tests/ollama-generation-contract.test.ts tests/groq-generation-contract.test.ts
  if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Generation contract tests failed' }
}
```

Expected planning result: strict check and 67 tests pass; no browser, actual credential, local runtime or provider access. Actual counts may change with a reconciled future tree; never impose a historical count as a runtime assertion.

### Bound command slots

The following index resolves to M602-G-CONTRACT-01 below. Its final research acceptance and A qualification are required before execution; the command table itself grants no premature operation.

| Slot | Required binding |
| --- | --- |
| IDENTITY | Intentional current HEAD/dirty paths, source/test/caller/config hashes, all 13 recursive frozen references, protected original runs/evidence, ordinary path topology and developer prerequisites; no private content output |
| OFFLINE | Exact focused commands/flags, injected doubles, deadlines, scratch and expected assertions; production extraction uses accepted Red/Green ownership |
| PREPARE | Minimal explicit task-owned output location, exclusive creation/permissions and inventory, exact build/scratch/application/browser setup, before/after mutation list; no lock/manifest regeneration or installation |
| LOCAL CASE | Exact one-case command for each Local profile, fixed runtime/model/settings, whole-message fit, metadata request bound, single dispatch, deadline, private evidence, in-call UI/OOM observation and normal stop |
| GROQ CASE | Exact one-case command for each Groq profile, public documentation/account-limit check, fixed credential source without exposure, manifest+amendment binding, measured/sent body, single dispatch, bounded result and cleanup |
| READBACK / RUBRIC | Validate recorded identity/outcome/proposal without execution; original-output binding, one-evaluator checks and tracked content-safe summaries |
| FULL | Current maintained sequential complete suite, accepted additions, independent strict/build, no concurrent service/browser use and capture/actual-case flags absent |
| CLEANUP / DOCS | Exact owned processes and disposable child paths only, ordinary-path/link/content guards and expected effects; preservation and environment restoration; links/status/format/PowerShell checks and git diff --check including separate untracked-file inspection |

Resolve dependencies before an affected lease; workers may not invent commands or credentials procedures. If a slot is unnecessary, mark it Not applicable with evidence. A metadata/account problem is not permission for a synthetic provider probe. No shell loop may execute the six real requests unattended.

## Validation and Acceptance

G/A acceptance requires complete frozen and canonical validation, tested no-effect failures, common output validation, honest configuration/wire identity, bounded attempt/deadline/cleanup and no credential/payload leakage. Receipts and worker summaries support, but do not replace, primary inspection of actual code, responsibility placement and evidence.

B acceptance requires exactly three Local and three Groq attempted fixed generation executions on the same unchanged packages, each with its original outcome and all required observations. Pre-call failures and not-run cases cannot fill the six-execution count. Every happy case must satisfy runtime validation and the frozen happy-case rubric; separately record each failure rather than computing a product score. Local calls must complete sequentially without OOM or unusable interface. Any material evaluated input/adapter/configuration change follows the new-binding/new-evidence authority before further output inspection; never mix an unacknowledged changed configuration into the six-case result.

Final acceptance additionally requires current authoritative regression, independent strict/build, preserved frozen/original evidence, correct private/tracked boundary, different final review and documentation closure. This task does not prove actual retrieval returned the controlled guidance, broad model quality, hosted complete input consumption, public accessibility, hardware support or release readiness.

## Idempotence and Recovery

Re-entry starts read-only: inspect current task/contract, original six-entry allowance, exclusive local records, actual effects and owned process state. Never infer no request from no response or missing result file. An uncertain operation stays consumed/unknown; do not replay it to discover what happened. Preserve partial records and use readback/offline investigation.

G must define a minimal durable consumption boundary before possible dispatch and how publication failure is reported. Prefer existing exclusive-file/directory primitives and six bounded records; do not invent a transactional job runner, retry graph or resumable generation subsystem. Code corrections do not replenish provider allowances. New calls, replacement cases or changed manifests require explicit owner/authority disposition and preserve earlier failed evidence.

Preserve all original `data/runs`, frozen corpus/evaluation artifacts and historical ignored evidence, including M6-01 failure and successful retrieval roots. No raw content enters tracked documentation. Stop only task-owned application/browser processes, restore their environment, then remove only verified ordinary disposable child paths named in G. Never recursively remove `temp`, `data/runs`, browser/model roots or unknown contents. Do not stop or restart the developer-owned Ollama runtime. Cleanup uncertainty stops advancement and remains visible.

Missing frozen files require byte-identical recovery under M3-01's existing contract or an owner-approved successor freeze, not reconstruction from memory. Missing runtime/account prerequisites are not implementation defects; finish unaffected work and request only the material missing prerequisite. No fallback or acquisition is implicit.

The builder uses descriptor-safe bounded readers, rejects duplicate/empty identifier domains and non-string/ill-formed text, and checks ordered context equality against JSON.parse of the detached user message's finding.findingId, finding.facts[].reference and guidance.passages[].passageId. It never treats arbitrary caller data as eligibility; the package remains the eligibility owner. Two messages with exact system/user roles are mandatory. Bounded strings/arrays use existing contract limits where applicable; final byte-fit remains mandatory. The builder alone creates/deep-freezes schema/request and privately registers the request identity. readCaseGenerationSchema rejects copies, foreign schemas/configurations and transplanted requests without caller schema serialization. No live package is admitted from arbitrary context.

Existing fit owners add prepareCaseOllamaGenerationWire and prepareCaseGroqGenerationWire alongside unchanged old exports, sharing only their current serialization/fit responsibilities. Exact issued requests and matching alternate singletons are required. Existing factory owners add createCaseOllamaGenerationAdapter(requestImplementation?,onRejection?) and createCaseGroqGenerationAdapter(options?) with the same collaborator types as existing exports. Private finite sharing reuses their current native/credential/cleanup bodies; no public arbitrary configuration or preparer callback. Configuration and invocation readers admit only the exact new schema tuple, retaining both old tuples. No ordinary production caller selects the new exports. Default adapters reject case requests; case adapters reject legacy or forged requests before credential/native effects. Candidate validation is untouched.

Return the same package contract plus caseSchemaSha256; identities.schemaSha256 remains explicitly the original M301 schema hash. Default loading checks the exact new body/schema pins above; injected packages derive their own authenticated identities through the same builder. New campaign manifest authentication additionally binds its schema/wire table to the derived six packages, including injected test manifests whose input hash and tables must match their synthetic authenticated packages. No default/test validation bypass or partial real-I/O fallback.

**S2 observer semantics:** prepareM602SuccessorObservers gains only an optional fourth policy discriminator 'm602-loading-observation-v2'; omission preserves all legacy scheduling and one-sample behavior. New campaign supplies it for qualification and actual Local observation. A valid absent response is a JSON object with a models array of object entries with nonempty string names, zero exact qwen3.5:4b names, valid HTTP/completeness/UTF8/byte limits and fully closed owned resources. Other malformed entries are terminal encoding-json; duplicate exact targets are target-cardinality. No schema/metadata relaxation. Absence is provisional until close, emits no failure, and is not a RuntimeSample. Acquisition cap/attempt exhaustion emits existing target-cardinality once before aborting owned work; per-request timeout remains transport-lifecycle. Cancellation keeps frozen aborted timing and null reconciled detail. Unknown cleanup blocks polling. GPU waits for runtime's successful acquisition settlement, then attempts its own gate only while active; it never starts after known native termination. Polling is observer metadata only and cannot create a generation request or change adapter metadata counts. Its finite resources join existing cleanup ownership. Qualification retains the existing7-second controlled window with actual UI and actual baseline GPU plus honestly synthetic no-op slots; its explicit runtime field prevents claims of live sampling proof.

### M602-DIAG2-CALLBACK-01 — Trusted callback boundary and runtime review disposition

Initial runtime S3 review returns REVISE with one Major F1: the packet overstates callback containment. Its public-seam reproduction returns a native rejected Promise with an own throwing constructor getter. The observer emits the correct encoding-json code, rejects, completes cleanup and removes its synthetic root, but Node24.20.0 then exits1 before normal completion. No global mutation or actual I/O is needed. This is a real limitation of the overbroad promise, not a historical runtime cause. All25 focused tests,22 baseline/current validator-and-read-order comparisons and six supported callback probes pass. No other implementation or cohesion defect is found.

Primary reconciles both optional diagnostic seams as trusted, promptly returning application-owned callbacks. They are never supplied by model/provider data. Supported containment includes synchronous exceptions, ordinary same-realm native fulfilled/rejected/pending Promises (including tested poisoned own then/catch methods), ordinary non-Promise thenable assimilation failures and reentrancy. Callers must preserve Promise constructor/species/prototype machinery and platform globals, own detached work/rejections, and avoid arbitrary destructive effects or synchronous blocking. No in-process callback API can isolate all such caller behavior. Corrupted constructor/species machinery is outside the guarantee and can leave a rejection unhandled; it is not reported as fixed or made safe by this phase.

The [ECMAScript PromiseResolve operation](https://tc39.es/ecma262/multipage/control-abstraction-objects.html#sec-promise-resolve) reads a native Promise's constructor before adoption, and [Promise.prototype.then](https://tc39.es/ecma262/multipage/control-abstraction-objects.html#sec-promise.prototype.then) resolves its species before attaching reactions. Together with the pinned Node reproduction, this supports the bounded interpretation; it is not a reason to mutate caller-owned Promises, intercept global rejections or introduce a general isolation framework. The untrusted candidate/HTTP boundaries remain fully validated, and privacy, verdicts, timing, cleanup, frozen inputs and acceptance requirements are unchanged.

This is primary-owned reconciliation of a new diagnostic API capability claim within the authorized phase. No requirement or accepted architecture is changed, no test assertion is removed, no unsupported case is called passing and no worker budget is renewed. Both acceptance paragraphs and the README API limitation are corrected. Implementation bytes stay unchanged. The runtime's sole complete correction review and the candidate's sole complete F1 correction review now assess the full reconciled packets; different final integrated review remains required.

```powershell
$m602RevisionBefore = [Environment]::GetEnvironmentVariable('A11Y_APPLICATION_REVISION', 'Process')
try {
  $m602Revision = (& git rev-parse HEAD).Trim()
  if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0 -or $m602Revision -notmatch '^[0-9a-f]{40}$') { throw 'Revision unavailable' }
  [Environment]::SetEnvironmentVariable('A11Y_APPLICATION_REVISION', $m602Revision, 'Process')
  Invoke-M105Command {
    & $m105Node tests/helpers/m602-run-case.ts --qualify-completion-observers
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Completion qualification stopped' }
  } $m105UiTemp
} finally {
  $m602RevisionRestore = if ($null -eq $m602RevisionBefore) { [System.Management.Automation.Language.NullString]::Value } else { $m602RevisionBefore }
  [Environment]::SetEnvironmentVariable('A11Y_APPLICATION_REVISION', $m602RevisionRestore, 'Process')
  if ([Environment]::GetEnvironmentVariable('A11Y_APPLICATION_REVISION', 'Process') -cne $m602RevisionBefore) { throw 'Revision environment restoration failed' }
}
```

Create two frozen message objects containing the exact authenticated instruction and input strings, including one final LF. Do not call the current `createGenerationRequest` for evaluation: it substitutes instructions and reserializes input. Require deep structural equality of the authenticated frozen schema with `GENERATION_SCHEMA`, then use that required object identity. Use unchanged configuration controls and runtime version fields in `GenerationRequest`; label these as adapter configuration, never actual message provenance. Preserve separate frozen manifest/instruction/input/schema hashes and measured actual body hash/bytes. No new production tuple is admitted.

| Profile | Local input upper bound | Local bytes / SHA-256 | Groq bytes / SHA-256 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| image | 17091 | 17748 / `4c13611115bca2dbf5d6d364a0e00d4bb03f40a5200322ad068bc4726306dfd6` | 17823 / `eb1b3dd6c8c9bf818836296fd33f05262eaa92318ac2160ca673dadf2a6c120a` |
| label | 18412 | 19141 / `4f8f218eaaf948af041fbec3eb119430f72342f53eb2faf2233366818f7ddf5a` | 19216 / `4957a37acc68b2b57ab35da25184a697b5358deb6978d8e58701ae2717b0d5c6` |

#### G contract: interfaces and responsibility placement

Only these production paths are implementation-owned:

- `src/server/generation/proposal-contract.ts`: extract `validateProposalCandidate(candidate, {findingId, availableEvidenceReferences, passageIds}): ProposalValidationResult`. Context references retain existing `EvidencePath` types. Preserve all descriptor/prototype/sparse-array/length/reference/lexical readers and detached frozen output. Existing `validateProposal` retains native/retrieval/support eligibility then delegates. The new candidate context is authenticated scope, not eligibility proof.
- New `src/server/generation/generation-execution.ts`: export `executeGenerationOperation({signal, providerContext, adapter?, admit, beforeTransport?})`. `admit(signal)` returns or resolves `failed/input-integrity`, existing `abstained/decision`, or `ready/{createRequest(configuration), validateCandidate(candidate)}`. Own the fixed 120000-ms total deadline beginning before admission, immutable bindings/fit, preparation, one-use capability, envelope reading, candidate validation and cleanup. Preserve current stage failure precedence and sticky duplicate behavior.
- `src/server/generation/generation-stage.ts`: retain public `executeGeneration` signature. Its admission closure owns corpus reads, native/retrieval/input eligibility and production request/validator context. Map the shared outcome back to unchanged `GenerationOutcome` and `ProviderInvocation`. No production import from `tests/`.

The shared outcome is a closed union: proposal with validated `Proposal`, `attempted:true`, observation and `cleanupFailed:false`; abstained with existing decision, `attempted:false`, no observation and `cleanupFailed:false`; failed with existing `GenerationErrorCode`, truthful `attempted`, optional observation only when attempted, and `cleanupFailed`. Observation contains only `{adapterConfiguration: Omit<ProviderInvocation,'outcome'|'validation'>, outcome: InvocationOutcome, validation: ProviderInvocation['validation']}`. No raw candidate/envelope/error/adapter escapes. Production alone maps configuration observation to persisted invocation.

`beforeTransport?: () => void` is a synchronous durable-marker callback. Exact order: reject inactive or used capability (duplicate stays sticky); consume capability; check stop/full bindings/callback type; invoke hook; require returned value `undefined`; check stop/full bindings/stop again; check final ordinary own-data adapter configuration descriptor identity; set attempted true and immediately invoke `start()` with no intervening callback. Throw, asynchronous return, expiry, reentry (even caught) or mutation cannot dispatch. Configuration failure applies unless shutdown/timeout has precedence. A marker means possible subsequent dispatch, never positive attempted proof. Finally deactivate capability before aborting the owned controller, then remove timer/listener, so prepared-undispatched credentials clear without reopening dispatch. Synchronous OS work is not forcibly interruptible; the post-hook deadline check blocks late dispatch.

Evaluation code is substantive Green-owned behavior, not a test fixture exception:

- `tests/helpers/m602-package.ts`: export `loadM602Package(caseLabel, environment?)`, performing the authentication/reconstruction above and returning the closed result defined below. The optional complete environment is an offline test seam; real CLI supplies no environment and uses the fixed manifest trust anchor and ordinary bounded filesystem reads. No HTTP, credentials or publication here.
- `tests/helpers/m602-operation.ts`: export `executeM602Case(caseLabel, dependencies?)` and `readM602Case(caseLabel, dependencies?)`. Own exactly one fixed operation, its exclusive records and native request accounting. Offline dependencies may provide an isolated synthetic root, byte/filesystem operations, clock and adapter/native doubles; actual CLI accepts no injection/root/configuration options. Reuse existing adapters, native transports and shared execution. No retry, scheduler or provider stack.
- `tests/helpers/m602-run-case.ts`: accept exactly `--execute --case <one-label>` or `--readback --case <one-label>`; no default, combined modes, duplicate flags or extra arguments. Invoke one helper and print only a bounded content-safe summary (identity, counts, outcome, hashes, closed error). Readback instantiates no adapter and reads no credential.

The existing stage-to-core and both caller-to-candidate-reader dependency edges are the only production interface change. Extract contiguous existing responsibilities; permitted structural refactor is limited to the named stage and candidate reader. New helpers separate frozen admission, effect/evidence coordination and CLI parsing. Test ownership: `tests/m602-generation.test.ts`, `tests/generation-stage.test.ts`, `tests/generation-contract.test.ts`. Code owner cannot change these accepted tests. Primary owns this plan, commands, assessments, README and status documents. No other source/test/config/dependency/frozen paths are authorized. A material cohesion problem returns to primary reconciliation rather than authorizing a broad helper.

No lease/write occurred. The following binds only the already-authorized offline injection seam and result interfaces; the real CLI still admits no override. Both environments execute the same authentication/reconstruction code, without a skip-validation branch. Complete revised-artifact G1–G6 review returned PASS with no findings at SHA-256 `ebfee0335f1fa6836706e55d54124fddfcab45a173f8dfcd45a3e3aed4b6ad6d`; primary accepts the reconciliation. One final-artifact correction cycle has been used, without renewing any research or execution allowance.

`M602CaseLabel` is exactly the six directory literals above. Export `M602PackageEnvironment` as `{readonly manifestSha256:string; readonly readBytes:(relativePath:string)=>Uint8Array}`. Both properties are mandatory when injected; no partial environment/default mixing. Its manifest digest must be a lowercase 64-character hex digest, its reader returns bounded bytes for exact verified relative paths, and every manifest/reference/provenance/canonical check still runs. Default environment privately binds the original fixed digest and guarded repository reader; it cannot be altered by environment variables, CLI flags or file contents. The test worker may generate a synthetic manifest and its digest from existing public native/corpus fixtures, and provide matching synthetic input/provenance/source/seed bytes in memory. Never copy ignored frozen payloads into tracked fixtures. Wrong trust anchor, inconsistent hashes or payloads still fail through the identical validator.

`loadM602Package` is synchronous and returns `{status:'failed',error:'input-integrity'}` or `{status:'ready',value:M602Package}`. `M602Package` has exactly `caseLabel`, `providerContext` (existing type), `findingId`, `availableEvidenceReferences` (readonly EvidencePath array), `passageIds` (readonly string array), `identities:{manifestSha256,inputSha256,instructionsSha256,schemaSha256,provenanceSha256}`, `wire:{sha256,bytes}`, `createRequest(configuration:GenerationConfiguration):GenerationRequest`, and `validateCandidate(candidate:unknown):ProposalValidationResult`. Freeze all returned containers. Message strings remain private in the request factory closure until requested. `wire` derives from unchanged provider preparers; default loading additionally requires the six fixed body identities in this contract. Injected synthetic identity uses its authenticated bytes and the same serialization/fit path, never a caller-supplied body digest.

Export `M602OperationDependencies` with optional `root:string`, `packageEnvironment:M602PackageEnvironment`, `requestImplementation:OllamaNativeRequest` (same callable shape serves both adapters), `credentialIO:GroqCredentialIO`, `signal:AbortSignal`, and `filesystem:Partial<Pick<typeof import('node:fs'),'mkdirSync'|'openSync'|'writeSync'|'fsyncSync'|'closeSync'|'lstatSync'|'realpathSync'|'readFileSync'|'readdirSync'>>`. Dependencies are trusted test collaborators, never serialized input. Default root is the fixed evidence root. An injected root must be an ordinary `temp/m602-test-<UUID>` child; require it whenever any other dependency is injected. No dependency may target original evidence/data/freeze/credential files. Test roots and supplied in-memory byte maps are isolated; default execution accepts no root override from process configuration. Clock/deadline tests use Node's existing test mock timers, not a new production clock abstraction. Adapters are always the existing factories; the native-request and credential-I/O seams inject their existing doubles. Filesystem injection exists solely for real write/flush/close/topology failure assertions; ordinary operation uses native functions.

Supplying any dependencies object, including `{}`, selects isolated test execution. Before creating a directory/record, require `root`, `packageEnvironment` and `requestImplementation`; Groq additionally requires `credentialIO`. Missing members return `evidence-blocked` with zero effects, never fallback to real native transport or credential I/O. Local never invokes credential I/O. Injected readback requires `root` and `packageEnvironment` only and never invokes request/credential collaborators. Real defaults are selected only when the dependencies argument is entirely absent, as on the strict direct CLI path. Test byte readers/credentials remain trusted in-memory collaborators; this is not a sandbox for malicious test code. Qualify every missing-collaborator variant with zero native/credential/directory calls.

Export `M602Result` and `M602Assessment` as the exact record shapes below. `executeM602Case` returns `Promise<{ok:true,result:M602Result}|{ok:false,error:'evidence-blocked'|'evidence-publication'}>`; a published failed result is `ok:true` with `result.status:'failed'`, and the CLI still exits nonzero. `readM602Case` returns `Promise<{ok:true,result:M602Result,assessment:M602Assessment|null}|{ok:false,error:'evidence-blocked'}>`; missing assessment is allowed for inspecting a just-finished result, but never for advancing. Neither helper leaks exception text. Readback ignores/never invokes request/credential collaborators, authenticates the package and validates the retained proposal with the common reader.

Export `parseM602Arguments(args:readonly string[])` from the CLI module, returning `{ok:true,mode:'execute'|'readback',caseLabel:M602CaseLabel}` or `{ok:false,error:'arguments'}`. Importing the module has no effects; only direct invocation enters the CLI. Its direct-entry path calls the helpers without dependencies. Tests may exercise the parser in process and invalid direct CLI arguments without creating a real case root.

#### G contract: records, privacy and continuation

Primary creates only the exact evidence root after ignore, ordinary-ancestor and inherited-ACL inspection. Existing case directories, including empty/malformed ones, permanently block that case. After checking prior-case acceptance and explicit label, execution exclusively creates its nonrecursive directory before package admission or adapter preparation. An operation is consumed at this directory creation. Each record uses exclusive creation, complete write, fsync and close; never overwrite, rename over, delete or repair a record. Files are bounded to 1 MiB, ordinary and single-link. Reject unexpected record names. Partial writes, flush/close failure and missing terminal records remain consumed/unknown and block every later case. These are conservative ordinary-filesystem guarantees, not power-loss durability or defense against malicious same-user mutation.

All JSON records have `version:'m602-evidence-v1'` and exact `caseLabel`. Hex digests are lowercase 64 characters; timestamps are UTC ISO strings. Unknown properties, inconsistent fields, invalid hashes/times/counts or malformed nested output invalidate readback. JSON is UTF-8, two-space indentation plus LF. Fixed record shapes:

| File / owner | Exact additional fields and meaning |
| --- | --- |
| `entered.json` / operation | `enteredAt`, `manifestSha256`, `mode`, `configurationSha256`, `codeSha256`. Configuration hashes JSON serialization of unchanged configuration; code hash is SHA-256 of sorted `{path,sha256}` entries for every `src/**/*.ts`, the three M602 helpers, `package.json`, `package-lock.json`, `tsconfig.json`. Entered publication completes before admission/preparation. |
| `dispatch.json` / hook | `enteredSha256`, `markedAt`, `meaning:'dispatch-may-have-started'`. Publication completes before native generation entry. |
| `result.json` / operation | `enteredSha256`, `dispatchSha256` (null if no complete marker), `finishedAt`, `status` (`proposal`, `abstained`, `failed`), `error` (existing closed GenerationErrorCode or null), `attempted`, `cleanupFailed`, `observation` (shared configuration observation or null), `wire` (`{sha256,bytes}` or null), `requests:{version,show,tags,chat}`, `chatWindow` (`{startedAt,finishedAt}` or null), `proposal` (validated original or null). `proposal` status requires attempted, one chat, exact wire and complete cleanup; no raw invalid candidate is retained. Abstention/failure never fills a completed generation count. |
| `assessment.json` / primary only | `resultSha256`, `evaluator:'primary'`, `assessedAt`, `dimensions` (one `{observation,value}` per manifest rubric row, in frozen order), `uncertainty` (`pass`, `fail`, `not-run`), `localObservation` (null for Groq; Local shape below), `accepted` (boolean). No freeform model text or private facts. |

Local observation shape is `{ui:{startedAt,finishedAt,responsive},runtime:{observedAt,digest,contextLength,sizeBytes,sizeVramBytes},gpuBefore:{observedAt,usedMiB,freeMiB},gpuDuring:{observedAt,usedMiB,freeMiB},oomObserved}`. Native chat starts immediately before real `http.request`/`https.request`; its finish is the first terminal request error/close or response end/close, recorded by passive listeners. UI start/end and runtime/GPU-during timestamps must lie strictly within this window. Missing/ambiguous timing fails observation; never extend the window to cover a late UI action. Request wrappers count fixed routes and hash actual `.end` body without retaining it or accessing/logging credential headers. No new transport behavior, redirects or retries.

On write/publication failure, retain every partial artifact, return nonzero and only a closed summary `evidence-publication`; do not create replacement evidence. An occupied/unverifiable prior path returns `evidence-blocked`, consumes no new directory and stops. CLI exit zero for execution means terminal valid proposal publication only; it does not mean semantic acceptance. Readback exit zero means records are internally valid, not that another call is allowed. Inconsistent/missing result is unknown even when no response was seen. Any marker is conservative possible-dispatch evidence; only live attempted accounting plus one native request supports a completed attempted-call claim.

Every next CLI requires all preceding cases' intact hash-linked proposal results and primary assessments with `accepted:true`, every required current-generation rubric dimension and uncertainty passing, and valid Local observation where applicable. Historical scanner provenance and actual retrieval relevance retain their original separate interpretation; they cannot be promoted to fresh scan/retrieval success. Assessments must use the exact frozen ten dimension names and values; `actual retrieval relevance` records `fail` for the preserved unsuitable current G1 and `not-run` for generation-only retrieval execution, with explanation in tracked result interpretation. Those historical dimensions do not masquerade as current-generation passing gates. Current-generation gates are evidence completeness, controlled support and citations, structural validity, semantic groundedness, remediation usefulness, human judgment and reminder, prohibited claims, provider completion, plus uncertainty and Local observation. No failed original output is repaired; no assessment overwrite or second semantic evaluator.

Credentials remain within the unchanged Groq loader/transport/echo filtering. No credential read in preflight/readback; no `.env` modification. Raw envelopes, hidden reasoning, invalid response content, full exception strings and request headers are never retained or printed. Original validated proposals stay only in ignored result files. Inherited Windows ACL inspection establishes known local access, not owner-only privacy; do not rely on mode 0600 or mutate ACLs. Tracked summaries contain only outcome/dimension labels, hashes, count and limitation.

#### G contract: exact commands and prerequisites

All commands use repository cwd, PowerShell 7.6.5, pinned Node v24.20.0 and the unchanged README preparation definitions. Load definitions only, never adjacent acquisition:

```powershell
$m602Readme = Get-Content README.md -Raw
$m602Prep = [regex]::Match($m602Readme, '(?s)### Development command preparation.*?```powershell\r?\n(.*?)\r?\n```').Groups[1].Value
if (-not $m602Prep) { throw 'Development preparation block missing' }
. ([scriptblock]::Create($m602Prep))
```

**IDENTITY / PREPARE:** `git status --short`, `git rev-parse HEAD`, check absent active lease, compare the entry inventory's protected hashes and lock/frozen identities. Hash the exact code list above and unchanged configurations before each actual case; compare with A-accepted values recorded here before B. The primary also pins relevant test/command identities at each evidence acceptance. Inspect root/ancestors with `Assert-M105OrdinaryPath`, `Get-Acl`, and `git check-ignore`; create only missing exact evidence root with `New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path 'temp/m602-generation-evidence' -ErrorAction Stop`. No case directories are precreated. Inspect existing three `dist/client` files against entry inventory before the ordinary Vite replacement build; any unknown file stops preparation. No dependency/model/browser acquisition.

**OFFLINE:** every test uses synthetic in-memory packages or isolated `temp/m602-test-<UUID>` roots and native doubles, never actual credentials/frozen operations. Focused runner:

```powershell
Invoke-M105Command {
  & $m105Node --test --test-timeout=120000 tests/m602-generation.test.ts tests/generation-contract.test.ts tests/generation-stage.test.ts tests/ollama-generation-contract.test.ts tests/ollama-generation.test.ts tests/groq-generation-contract.test.ts tests/groq-generation.test.ts
  if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'M6-02 focused verification failed' }
  & $m105Node node_modules/typescript/bin/tsc --project tsconfig.json
  if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Independent strict TypeScript failed' }
}
```

The Red command runs the same test list; the expected new-module/export failure may be accepted only under the first-module exception after preflight and independently established runner readiness. Blocked assertions are not claimed executed. Green must execute every unchanged test and strict check successfully.

After Green and before A review, primary also runs this read-only real-package check through `Invoke-M105Command`, piping JavaScript to `& $m105Node --input-type=module` and requiring exit 0. This binds the default trust anchor/wire table without an operation entry, adapter preparation, credential read or request. It is separate from portable default regression and requires the retained frozen files. The loop authenticates bytes only; it executes no generation.

```javascript
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { loadM602Package } from './tests/helpers/m602-package.ts';
for (const label of ['local-image','local-label','local-contrast','groq-image','groq-label','groq-contrast']) {
  const result = loadM602Package(label);
  assert.equal(result.status,'ready');
  console.log(JSON.stringify({caseLabel:label,identities:result.value.identities,wire:result.value.wire}));
}
```

**LOCAL CASE / GROQ CASE:** each line below is a separate manually authorized step after preceding assessment, never one pasted batch. Wrap each in `Invoke-M105Command`, test `$LASTEXITCODE`, and stop on nonzero. No extra actual-call flags or alternate parameters are permitted.

```powershell
& $m105Node tests/helpers/m602-run-case.ts --execute --case local-image
& $m105Node tests/helpers/m602-run-case.ts --execute --case local-label
& $m105Node tests/helpers/m602-run-case.ts --execute --case local-contrast
& $m105Node tests/helpers/m602-run-case.ts --execute --case groq-image
& $m105Node tests/helpers/m602-run-case.ts --execute --case groq-label
& $m105Node tests/helpers/m602-run-case.ts --execute --case groq-contrast
```

Local uses unchanged `QWEN_CONFIGURATION`, the exact digest above, Ollama 0.33.3, Q4_K_M, 32768 context, 4096 reserve and current template/parser identities. Adapter-owned metadata allows at most one each version/show/tags within each operation, then at most one chat; failed preparation is consumed without a generation execution. Automatic offload is observed, not tuned. Groq uses unchanged `GROQ_CONFIGURATION`, fixed official endpoint and exact model, `m304-groq-request-bytes-v1` and `groq-gpt-oss-20b-2026-09-11-v1` exposed-default identity, one chat and no metadata probe. Existing private credential loader runs only after entered marker inside actual preparation. Public current model/strict/deprecation docs and non-secret account availability/request/token limits must be established before Groq; unknown account readiness blocks those cases without a synthetic request.

**LOCAL OBSERVATION:** before the first Local case, start one task-owned application with the exact README `Run the local service` PowerShell block, ordinary empty scanner scratch, actual HEAD revision and port 0. Use its returned ready URL in a dedicated Chrome tab via CUA; do not activate Analyze or any retrieval/generation control. During each chat, focus the target textbox and change the Local/Groq radio; verify focused/checked accessibility state and record UTC bounds. This is responsiveness observation, not broad accessibility qualification. Missing the native window stops subsequent cases without repeating.

Take one GPU sample immediately before each Local operation and one during its chat, with timestamp around the command; require one finite row, nonnegative numbers, successful exit. Use `C:\Windows\system32\nvidia-smi.exe --query-gpu=memory.used,memory.free --format=csv,noheader,nounits`. Record memory observations without adding capacity thresholds.

Exactly one additional in-call Local metadata GET is allowed, only to `http://127.0.0.1:11434/api/ps`. Execute the following through `Invoke-M105Command`, piping this JavaScript to `& $m105Node --input-type=module`; it prints only the approved projection. No redirect, retry, credential or raw body output. A failed observation stops continuation even if generation completes.

```javascript
import http from 'node:http';
const expected = '2a654d98e6fba55d452b7043684e9b57a947e393bbffa62485a7aac05ee4eefd';
let settled = false;
const finish = (ok, value) => {
  if (settled) return; settled = true; clearTimeout(timer);
  console.log(JSON.stringify(ok ? value : {error:'local-observation'}));
  if (!ok) process.exitCode = 1;
};
const req = http.get('http://127.0.0.1:11434/api/ps', res => {
  const chunks = []; let size = 0;
  if (res.statusCode !== 200 || !/^application\/json(?:\s*;|$)/i.test(res.headers['content-type'] ?? '')) {
    res.destroy(); req.destroy(); finish(false); return;
  }
  res.on('data', chunk => { size += chunk.length; if (size > 65536) { res.destroy(); req.destroy(); finish(false); } else chunks.push(chunk); });
  res.on('error', () => finish(false));
  res.on('end', () => {
    try {
      const data = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(Buffer.concat(chunks)));
      const matches = data.models.filter(m => m.name === 'qwen3.5:4b');
      if (matches.length !== 1) throw new Error();
      const m = matches[0];
      if (m.digest !== expected || m.context_length !== 32768 || !Number.isSafeInteger(m.size) || m.size < 0 || !Number.isSafeInteger(m.size_vram) || m.size_vram < 0) throw new Error();
      finish(true,{observedAt:new Date().toISOString(),digest:m.digest,contextLength:m.context_length,sizeBytes:m.size,sizeVramBytes:m.size_vram});
    } catch { finish(false); }
  });
});
req.on('error', () => finish(false));
const timer = setTimeout(() => { req.destroy(); finish(false); },5000);
```

**READBACK / RUBRIC:** use the same wrapper with `& $m105Node tests/helpers/m602-run-case.ts --readback --case <exact-label>` (substitute only one of the six fixed literals). Read original validated proposal locally and evaluate every material claim against frozen facts/passages; never paste its content into tracked documentation/chat. Primary publishes the exact assessment object with Node `openSync(path,'wx')`, complete write, `fsyncSync`, `closeSync`, then hashes/readbacks it. Failure preserves partial bytes and blocks continuation. No other role writes assessments or semantically rescores outputs. Independent reviewers audit evidence and method.

**FULL:** after last accepted executable change, run the exact maintained README sequential 46-file suite plus `tests/m602-generation.test.ts` in its browser-free list; all existing module-mock flags and scratch assignments remain unchanged. Capture/actual flags must be absent. Run independent strict TypeScript and `& $m105Node node_modules/vite/bin/vite.js build --configLoader native` through the wrapper after approved build-inventory check. No concurrent app/browser test. Reuse fresh complete evidence only under the full workflow identity rule.

**CLEANUP / DOCS:** stop only the owned application with stdin `stop`, require service-stopped/exit 0 and exact environment restoration; close only its CUA tab. Do not manage Ollama. Test cleanup may remove only its exact ordinary synthetic `temp/m602-test-<UUID>` child after ancestry/content verification, using native PowerShell `Remove-Item -LiteralPath` if coordinator cleanup is needed. Require existing managed scratch roots empty before/after; preserve the evidence root, all case records, all original inventory roots, data/runs and freeze. Check protected hashes, actual Git diff/untracked files, local links/anchors, PowerShell syntax, UTF-8/newline/status consistency and `git diff --check`. Any unexpected mutation or cleanup uncertainty stops advancement.

#### G contract: A proof packet and stop rules

One standard, nonvisual slice `A-fixed-case-execution` under workflow `m602-six-fixed-generation-executions`; meaningful behavior requires test-worker preflight and Red, then separate code-worker Green under exact leases. Maximum chain: initial plus one correction, conditional third write only with documented progress/new evidence; one review correction loop. No budget renews a real case. S3 triggers are private egress, immutable identity, single-use dispatch and uncertain-result recovery. A requires fresh critical implementation review; C requires a different fresh critical reviewer.

Negative/positive evidence must cover G1–G6: all hash/newline/order/case/schema/provenance/canonical faults with zero native calls; complete input reconstruction and full notices; common candidate unknown fields/getters/proxies/sparse arrays/invented references/lexical/secret-echo rejection and frozen detached output; exact native body identity/bytes and fixed controls/routes; Local fit versus Groq byte admission; occupied/partial/reparse/hardlink/invalid records; complete-write/flush/close/terminal-publication failures; prior assessment gating; marker throw/reentry/caught duplicate/config mutation/async return/deadline; shutdown, late settlement and uncertain cleanup; prepared Groq clearing; readback with zero credential/adapter effects. Preserve existing stage tests including attempts/reentrancy around lines 707–869. Default tests use controlled doubles only and never require private frozen files.

Any failed/invalid/unknown case, semantic failure, missing Local observation, OOM/unusable interface, drift, secret exposure or uncertain cleanup stops subsequent generation. Finish unaffected offline verification/documentation and ask only for the missing owner prerequisite/disposition. Do not reopen mechanism selection merely because a model fails. Reopen the contract if production eligibility/provenance changes, frozen inputs cannot pass unchanged adapters, shared extraction changes existing failure semantics, or a broader framework becomes necessary. Completion remains contingent on all six actual executions and task Verification; these contract reviews cannot substitute for them.

### M602-A-PREFLIGHT-01 — Reconciled missing behavior and Red packet

Read-only preflight confirms existing hardened candidate reading and covered stage behavior, but no exported candidate-only reader, shared execution callable/hook, frozen-package loader or one-case evidence/CLI mechanism. Its initial CONFLICTING result identified the two test-interface issues resolved by the reviewed G amendment. Workflow `m602-six-fixed-generation-executions`, slice `A-fixed-case-execution`, assignment `m602-a-red-01`, owner `m602-test-worker`, phase `red`, attempt 1, correction parent None. Standard nonvisual S3; TDD applicable. Exact acceptance, callable types, placement/dependency/refactor constraints and G1–G6 negative packet are the accepted contract above. Allowed files are only `tests/m602-generation.test.ts`, `tests/generation-stage.test.ts`, `tests/generation-contract.test.ts`; no allowed directory root. All application source, substantive helpers, documentation, configuration/dependency/frozen files, retained evidence and Git mutations are forbidden. The UI observation began 19927 ms after chat termination; the nominal during-call GPU sample and runtime GET followed termination by 63771 ms and 63860 ms respectively. The pre-call GPU sample remains a pre-call sample; late responsiveness and residency cannot prove in-call usability or absence of OOM.
