### M602-FOLLOWUP-01 — Offline follow-up authorization and decision contract

**Owner authorization and scope:** Following the diagnosis, the owner explicitly authorized the proposed bounded work: content-free failure reasons, improved observation coordination, offline tests and independent review, with no real generation. This is additional M6-02 scope and a new explicitly authorized allowance for that scope, not a relabeling or renewal of the exhausted A chain. Original research/test/Green receipts, failures and budgets remain historical and unchanged. Work is standard/nonvisual: no rendered UI change, provider configuration, prompt/schema/corpus change, dependency acquisition, credential access, model/runtime request, real case entry, assessment or retry. Existing Local image evidence must remain byte-identical, and all five later cases remain stopped.

**Decision Review Contract — M602-FUP-G:** R3, because diagnostic information crosses untrusted response/credential boundaries, new observation coordination has lifetime/concurrency semantics, and retained evidence compatibility has custom integrity/recovery constraints. Target: one bounded follow-up contract in this ExecPlan before writes. The original G remains the authority for its original records and failed campaign; follow-up code must neither reinterpret that failure nor silently admit another original case. Production persistence and renderer contracts stay unchanged unless a demonstrated requirement returns for explicit scope reconciliation.

**Question and fixed candidates:** Compare (A) content-free optional reporting callbacks with task-local diagnostic/observation evidence separate from existing result shapes, and (B) optional closed diagnostic data on execution outcomes with an explicitly versioned evaluation record. Both must use the existing adapters, shared validator/executor and native chat boundary. Compare only these direct placements; introduce no general telemetry, scheduler, alternate provider stack or raw-output store. Observation coordination must have an actual invocation seam, trigger from native chat lifetime, reject observations outside that lifetime, and be provable using synthetic callbacks. It must not merely export an unused helper or claim that manual tool latency is bounded. Choose the smallest compatible implementation, including a narrower combination if required by current responsibility boundaries.

**Common criteria and hard gates:** useful deterministic rejection classification without content leakage; unchanged validation/provider behavior; bounded callback failures and lifecycle; trustworthy timestamps and no observation request after known terminal state; preservation and honest readback of the original failed record after code changes; no silent migration or resumed real execution; cohesive modules and behavioral tests; no dependencies or speculative generalization. Decide diagnostic transport, finite reason vocabulary, evidence version/compatibility, observer invocation/terminal rules and exact allowed paths before Green. Prove actual race handling, zero native/credential effects in offline tests, privacy, backward preservation and readback under implementation; defer only real-runtime effectiveness because real calls are outside this authorization.

**Cumulative invariant packet:** F1 closed content-free diagnostics on all relevant rejected-response paths, including hostile values; F2 unchanged production admission/outcome/wire/credential behavior; F3 original-record preservation, explicit version/code identity and no renewed entry; F4 start/terminal/abort concurrency and late-observer refusal; F5 caller-integrated observation completion with strict native-window evidence and no fabricated success; F6 cohesive ownership, complete test scope, authoritative status and evidence honesty. Every contract review covers all six, not just the latest correction.

**Routing, bounds and ownership:** One `critical_researcher` investigates the coupled response-reporting/evidence/lifetime dimension, initial report plus at most one bounded follow-up. Primary concurrently owns current authorities, original-record preservation and command/readiness binding. One `decision_analyst` synthesizes (initial plus one bounded correction), then fresh pre-draft and different final `critical_research_reviewer` barriers apply under the existing R3 rules. Primary authors the contract; no research role writes files. No optional drafter. The authorized implementation allowance is one coherent follow-up slice with separate test/code owners: one preflight, initial Red/Green and at most one ordinary correction each; a conditional third write requires the existing documented conditions. One S3 implementation correction-review loop and different final integration review apply. None of these allowances renews a real operation. Stop for changed material scope, an unresolved hard gate, repeated decisive failure without new evidence or exhausted allowance; preserve completed authorized work.

**Current baseline:** The original accepted code/tree identities and three failed-case hashes in M602-B-RESULT-01/M602-DIAGNOSIS-RESULT-01 are the entry preservation baseline. Exact follow-up command/test identities and path projections will be pinned in the Milestone Assignment Packet v2 after the reviewed contract. No active write lease exists; prior edits remain part of the baseline rather than unrelated changes to discard.

### M602-FUP-CONTRACT-01 — Offline diagnostics and observation contract

**Selection and evidence:** Primary selects candidate A: optional content-free rejection sinks and separate versioned timing/diagnostic evidence. FUPR01–07 identify the existing rejection, native accounting and strict readback boundaries; FUPR08–09 resolve linked-result compatibility and independent pending-work accounting. Candidate B would change closed execution unions without a demonstrated mandatory-return need. A preserves those unions and confines the additional evidence to the evaluation caller. Reverse this selection only if implementation demonstrates that these seams cannot meet containment or a mandatory outcome contract becomes necessary; do not expand scope implicitly. The original response remains indeterminate.

The initial pre-draft review found FUP-PD-01 (missing diagnostic/result compatibility) and FUP-PD-02 (ambiguous fault/pending precedence). The sole remaining researcher follow-up and primary reconciliation resolved both; bounded pre-draft recheck returned **PASS**, with no outstanding findings across F1–F6. Research and analyst allowances are now exhausted. Primary independently reproduced an additional source-order path with the existing native double: an invalid envelope followed by a response error before close returns `network` with uncertain cleanup. That one-double-call, no-network/no-write probe supplements and supersedes FUPR08's unamended exhaustive-matrix claim. A different fresh final research reviewer must accept this authored contract before preflight or a write lease.

**Scope:** Implement and qualify this contract offline only. Preserve original G result and assessment shapes, all model/prompt/schema/wire/fit/credential behavior and the original three failed-case files. No real generation, runtime metadata, browser/service startup, acquisition, original-root mutation, assessment, retry, successor campaign or later roadmap task is authorized. An observation timing receipt is not a UI/resource sample or a rubric assessment.

#### Rejection reporting and compatibility

`src/server/generation/generation-diagnostics.ts` owns the closed `GenerationRejectionCode` and `GenerationRejectionSink = (event: Readonly<{ code: GenerationRejectionCode }>) => unknown`, safe constant emission and finite-value reading. Codes are exactly `adapter-response/body`, `adapter-response/envelope`, `adapter-response/content`, `candidate/contract`, `executor/envelope`, `caller/correspondence` and `adapter-response/unspecified`. Body covers existing byte bounds/framing/UTF-8/outer-JSON rejection; envelope covers existing model/completion/role/thinking/extension predicates; content covers content-JSON/nonobject rejection. No exception text, rejected value, raw body, header, capability, credential or sample payload enters an event.

Add optional last parameters to `createOllamaGenerationAdapter(requestImplementation?, onRejection?)`, `dispatchOllamaGeneration(body, signal, attemptTransport, requestImplementation?, onRejection?)` and `validateProposalCandidate(candidate, context, onRejection?)`. Add optional `onRejection` to executor options and an optional second sink parameter to ready admission/package `validateCandidate(candidate, onRejection?)`. The authenticated package closure forwards that sink. Existing return unions, public production persistence and admission remain unchanged; omitted sinks preserve existing behavior. No Groq HTTP change: its rejected response uses the generic executor code.

Emit only newly owned frozen constant events. Do not await sinks. Contain synchronous throws, throwing thenable access and eventual asynchronous rejection without inspecting or stringifying the thrown value. This cannot forcibly bound a trusted synchronous callback. Executor envelope reporting surrounds only returned-envelope descriptor/closed-shape parsing, after duplicate and attempted guards; dispatch rejection, native throw, gate failure or cancellation does not acquire that code through the broader catch. Emit generic adapter rejection after `observe('response', 'failed')` and before the existing stop check. Candidate fallback is emitted after the failed candidate observation and before the stop check; the candidate validator may already have emitted the same code. Caller correspondence reporting occurs only when the existing proposal-to-failure conversion occurs. Preserve all validation predicates, transport counts, cleanup and terminal precedence.

The task-local collector processes events only until closure. No events yields complete integrity and null code. Repeated identical valid codes retain that code. The sole permitted mixed set is generic adapter rejection plus exactly one detailed adapter code; choose the detailed code independent of arrival order. Unknown/malformed events, every other mixed set or incompatibility with the linked result latch failed integrity and null code permanently. Late events cannot revise a frozen report. Diagnostic integrity never changes the generation result.

Every non-null code requires a validated linked result with `attempted:true`, `status:'failed'`, non-null observation and null proposal. In this table, R/F means observation `response/failed`, P/N means `provider/not-run`, C/N means the final cancellation error with `not-run`, and N/N means `network/not-run`.

| Code | Allowed final error | Observation | `cleanupFailed` |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Detailed adapter body/envelope/content | `response-validation` | R/F | Either |
| Detailed adapter body/envelope/content | `timeout` or `shutdown` | R/F | Either |
| Detailed adapter body/envelope/content | `timeout` or `shutdown` | C/N or N/N | True |
| Detailed adapter body/envelope/content | `network` | N/N | True |
| `adapter-response/unspecified` | `response-validation`, `timeout` or `shutdown` | R/F | Either |
| `candidate/contract` | `response-validation`, `timeout` or `shutdown` | R/F | False |
| `executor/envelope` | `provider` | P/N | True |
| `executor/envelope` | `timeout` or `shutdown` | P/N or C/N | True |
| `caller/correspondence` | `response-validation` | `response/passed` | False |

Caller correspondence additionally requires chat count unequal to one, absent native window, or actual wire hash/bytes different from the authenticated package. No other pairing is accepted. These are rejection-stage observations, not proof of the eventual terminal cause. The detailed-code network rows preserve the reproduced late-resource-error path and possible cancellation after its observation.

#### Observation lifecycle and timing-only evidence

`tests/helpers/m602-observation.ts` owns one coordinator per operation. Its kinds are exactly `ui`, `runtime` and `gpu`. `M602Observers` is a partial map from kind to `(gate: { signal: AbortSignal; start(effect: (signal: AbortSignal) => void | Promise<void>): boolean }) => void | Promise<void>`. The observer receives no native transport capability. Each effect initiates synchronously when admitted; its promise denotes observed settlement. A delayed observer calls `gate.start` after its wait, allowing refusal at the actual effect boundary.

`executeM602Case` creates and consumes this coordinator through `accountNative`. Native accounting registers first-terminal listeners before scheduling supplied Local observers, after native request construction. It does not await observers or delay request progress. Groq invokes none. A synchronous native terminal prevents later scheduling. Each kind's start capability is consumed before callback-capable work; immediately recheck active/abort before invoking the effect. Duplicate/reentrant/late starts return false with no second effect and latch refusal. Capture actual effect entry and settlement timestamps; do not fabricate an end from abort time.

Track observer pending and effect pending independently, plus fault and refusal. First native terminal, caller abort or operation finish closes once, revokes gates and freezes slots **before** invoking abort listeners. Own eventual promise rejection/settlement without indefinite waiting or modifying frozen evidence; detach owned listeners. At closure choose each configured slot in this order: pending observer or effect means aborted; otherwise any observed fault means failed; otherwise no effect, refused start, absent known native end or noninterior/ambiguous timing means missed-window; otherwise completed. Missing observer means unavailable. Successful effect settlement alone does not hide a still-pending observer.

Each slot has exactly `status`, `startedAt`, `finishedAt` and `cleanup`. Status is `unavailable | missed-window | failed | aborted | completed`; timestamps are canonical ISO strings or null. No effect means null/null. An effect has a non-null start and has a finish only if its settlement was observed while open. A finish requires a start and must be nondecreasing. Aborted always has uncertain cleanup and may have null/null, start/null or start/finish depending on the independent pending work. Unavailable is null/null with complete cleanup. Failed and missed-window have complete cleanup and either null/null or an observed start/finish pair. Completed has complete cleanup and both timestamps strictly inside the known native start/end; equality fails. Malformed or backward timestamps cannot produce accepted evidence. Cleanup describes observed callback/effect settlement, not proof that an external process or model stopped.

The report's aggregate observation cleanup is uncertain if and only if any slot is uncertain. At publication its `chatWindow` copies the authoritative result window exactly; slot states and timestamps remain frozen from coordinator closure. If caller abort preceded native termination, a later result window never upgrades a missed/aborted slot. Thus a missed-window slot may be interior to the later result window. A null result window permits no completed slot. Missing default observers explicitly remain unavailable; this scope adds no real observer implementation or synthetic fallback to a real runtime.

#### Supplemental storage and historical inspection

Extend `M602OperationDependencies` with optional `followup: { reportRoot: string; observers?: M602Observers }`. Existing injected callers omitting it retain their old result shape and behavior. Injected follow-up requires `reportRoot` equal to `<dependencies.root>/followup-evidence`, with existing synthetic root/package/native and Groq credential-double requirements; reject a mismatched binding before entry. Without dependencies, prospective execution binds only `temp/m602-followup-evidence-v1`, with observers unavailable. That default is a storage choice, not renewed execution authority.

`M602FollowupReport` is the closed object `{ version:'m602-followup-v1', caseLabel, producerCodeSha256, manifestSha256, enteredSha256, dispatchSha256, resultSha256, diagnostic:{ integrity:'complete'|'failed', code:GenerationRejectionCode|null }, observation:{ chatWindow, ui, runtime, gpu, cleanup:'complete'|'uncertain' } }`. Hashes are canonical SHA-256; dispatch hash and window may be null, as in the linked result. Failed diagnostic integrity requires null code. No arbitrary strings, sample contents, result copies or extra fields are allowed.

After successful original-shaped result publication, publish exactly `<reportRoot>/<enteredSha256>/report.json`. Create only the bound report root when needed and then the identity directory exclusively; use ordinary ancestor/endpoint checks, bounded canonical JSON, exclusive `wx`, full writes, fsync, close and exact byte readback. Do not overwrite, repair, delete residue or append a file to a case directory. Return the existing success fields plus optional `followup:{ report, sha256 }` only when reporting is enabled. A supplemental failure returns existing `evidence-publication` while preserving the already published result. Partial supplemental evidence remains blocked and never renews entry.

`readM602Followup(caseLabel, dependencies?)` returns `{ok:true,report,sha256}` or `{ok:false,error:'evidence-blocked'}`. It performs no preparation, native/credential call, observation or write. Injected readback requires explicit follow-up root. Reuse the internal strictly validated current source-record snapshot, retaining package/configuration/current-code checks. Derive the report directory from the authenticated entered hash; require exactly `report.json`, canonical bounded bytes, closed schema, the compatibility matrix, slot coherence and exact manifest/producer/entered/dispatch/result linkage. It provides no assessment or continuation eligibility and is never used to advance the sequence.

`readOriginalM602Case()` has no root or producer-hash override. It reads only the original Local image directory and requires exactly its three original files and full pinned identities: manifest `63770583a97e1d0517337474dadc706c1047d78e026e22c7a745ce7674ed9e6b`; producer `a069571f11959ba95670be9142169962262e834d5c9c81791ed90bae191522b4`; entered `1bc66fd5cb9f437b83c13403da7872392893871e19ad7478f6a53d50e51c272f`; dispatch `4d2b186092c95a1b35ca8f9bf9c748386a5b117ddb08b6e1a4dfc0fcb4544320`; result `05e6b16b86f676b090d9cdc06d3a4a151c2cfc8bc36fb9fcd788f558ae48d989`. It returns a closed success with `ok:true`, `kind:'historical-only'`, `eligibleForContinuation:false`, `caseLabel:'local-image'`, `status:'failed'`, `error:'response-validation'`, `attempted:true` and those five identity fields, or `evidence-blocked`. Verify current manifest bytes and pinned original producer/record links; do not require current code equality for this separate historical inspection. Never revalidate a historical proposal, create an assessment, migrate records or call this reader from execution/continuation. Current `readM602Case` remains strict.

Expose historical inspection as exactly `--inspect-original --case local-image` in the existing CLI, with a content-free summary and exit zero only for authenticated historical inspection. Other cases/options are rejected. Original execute/readback semantics stay unchanged. New helper files all join the operation-code fingerprint; historical inspection alone retains the original producer pin.

**Question and fixed candidates:** Compare (A) a narrow successor entry point composing the existing authenticated package, operation, diagnostic and observation boundaries with a literal new campaign binding, and (B) a narrowly extended existing operation/CLI with an explicit literal successor mode. Both must reuse the existing execution, validation, filesystem and lifecycle policies. An option that cannot qualify bounded, genuine in-call evidence is eliminated regardless of convenience.

**Criteria and hard gates:** immutable six input/wire/control identities; unambiguous separate campaign/code/evidence linkage; one-use exclusive entry and first-failure stop; no cross-campaign predecessor acceptance; real UI responsiveness and validated runtime/GPU samples strictly within native lifetime; no new effect initiation after known terminal state, honest failed/missed/aborted evidence and separately confirmed owned cleanup; unchanged credential and minimized egress boundary; smallest cohesive implementation/procedure with no dependency acquisition. Already initiated browser/HTTP/process work may settle after cancellation; such a sample cannot qualify or be retroactively moved inside the native window. Report Local and Groq separately, no quality score or ranking. The original failure remains visible even if a successor case succeeds.

**Decide now / prove later:** Decide exact literal identity/root/CLI and manifest relationship, prior-case eligibility, observer provisioning and ready/dispatch/terminal ordering, bounded sample shapes and assessment linkage, cleanup/uncertainty rules, ownership and commands before code or live effects. Implementation proves those semantics with native/credential/filesystem doubles and controlled short-lived observation windows. Before the first generation, primary must separately prove the actual application/browser and available sampling commands are ready using a no-generation procedure defined by the reviewed contract. That proof grants no warm-up, embedding, scan, retrieval, synthetic model request or extra runtime metadata poll. Runtime model admission remains inside each actual operation; current Groq public/account readiness remains an execution prerequisite. Do not defer a mechanism choice as a runtime experiment.

#### Interfaces, placement and test isolation

Preserve `executeM602Case`, `readM602Case`, `readM602Followup`, original CLI branches and historical-only inspection semantics. Add the following exports to `tests/helpers/m602-operation.ts`:

- `executeM602SuccessorCase(caseLabel: M602CaseLabel, dependencies?: M602SuccessorDependencies)`: resolves `{ok:true,result:M602SuccessorResult,observation:M602SuccessorObservation,observationSha256:string}` or `M602SuccessorFailure`. A valid retained failed/unqualified result may return `ok:true`; this never means case acceptance.
- `readM602SuccessorCase(caseLabel, dependencies?)`: resolves the same success fields plus `assessment:M602SuccessorAssessment|null`, or `{ok:false,error:'evidence-blocked'}`. No service/browser/observer, adapter, credential, native request or write is created.
- `qualifyM602SuccessorObservers(dependencies?)`: resolves `{ok:true,qualification:M602SuccessorQualification,sha256:string}`, or the same closed execution error union. Existing valid qualification is inspected/reused, never overwritten; an occupied invalid/partial file blocks. Qualification is not a case and consumes no generation entry.

Export `M602SuccessorFailure` from the evidence owner as exactly `{ok:false,error:'evidence-blocked'|'evidence-publication'|'observer-readiness',cleanup:M602SuccessorCleanup}`. Execution and qualification preserve this receipt on every failure, including missing dependencies, already-aborted admission, pre-entry preparation failure and partial source/observation/qualification publication. Before any resource acquisition, all cleanup fields are not-created; initiated but unresolved acquisition is uncertain at cleanup expiry. A failure after acquisition returns the frozen receipt from that invocation's owned cleanup. The CLI emits exactly these closed fields and exits nonzero; it must not collapse them to a generic error. This returned receipt is control evidence, not a replacement durable case record, an assessment or authority to overwrite residue. Primary may resume pre-entry readiness only after inspecting confirmed cleanup; any uncertain field or unavailable receipt prevents further live effects until reconciled. Readback remains read-only with its original closed error return above.

The CLI owner additionally exports the pure `formatM602SuccessorFailure(failure:M602SuccessorFailure):string`, with a type-only import from the evidence owner. It returns one compact JSON object plus one LF, selecting exactly `ok:false`, the bounded `error` and the six named `cleanup` properties. It never spreads arbitrary input properties, performs I/O, dispatches an operation or mutates process state. The actual successor execute/qualification failure branches write this function's return to stdout and set exitCode 1. This narrow formatter is the isolated proof boundary for CLI failure receipts; tests cover complete, not-created and uncertain cleanup, all three errors, extra-property exclusion, final newline and input immutability. Primary's actual-diff inspection verifies both branches use the formatter with nonzero exit and no alternate raw-output path. That branch wiring uses proportional structural evidence, not a new live runner, loader, mocking flag or default-resource test. Existing original and readback-only error behavior remains unchanged.

The private shared core owns exclusive entry, authenticated admission, adapter/native accounting, exactly one timing coordinator, dispatch and result publication. It returns an internal frozen snapshot of source hashes, result, diagnostics and timing. The original wrapper retains its original supplemental publication; the successor wrapper performs owned cleanup then publishes the successor observation. Do not create two coordinators or publish an original supplemental report for successor execution. Original paths, schemas, public return unions and defaults remain unchanged. Shared validators may accept a private discriminator containing only the two fixed record versions; validate every original property and every successor extension before returning, never strip unchecked properties to manufacture an original record.

New `tests/helpers/m602-successor-observers.ts` owns actual provisioning, UI/runtime/GPU collectors and resource cleanup. New `tests/helpers/m602-successor-evidence.ts` owns successor manifest, qualification, observation and assessment-projection validation. Operation remains evidence/effect coordination; browser/HTTP/child-process implementation does not move into it. Type-only imports of operation record types are permitted; no runtime circular dependency. Reuse existing provider, package, timing and filesystem modules. No application-source responsibility changes or production imports of evaluation code. Code fingerprint adds both new helpers and the new manifest to all current inputs; no other executable extraction is permitted without reconciliation.

Export successor record types from the evidence owner. `M602SuccessorResult` has precisely the original result fields with version `m602-successor-evidence-v1`; it does not replace the old result type. `M602SuccessorDependencies` is a new type in the operation owner with the existing test fields `root`, `packageEnvironment`, `requestImplementation`, `credentialIO`, `signal`, `filesystem`, plus `successorManifestEnvironment:M602PackageEnvironment` and `observerIO:M602SuccessorObserverIO`; fields may be optional in the TypeScript type to test missing-dependency rejection. It has no original `followup` option. A supplied object, including empty, selects isolated testing permanently. Require root, package environment and successor manifest environment for all operations; execution additionally requires the native double and Groq's credential double. Local execution and qualification additionally require the complete observer I/O set. Missing members fail before entry/resources; no actual factory fallback. Readback never invokes collaborators and requires no request/observer/credential double.

An injected root remains an ordinary `temp/m602-test-<UUID>` child, with test-owned `runs`, `client` and `observer-scratch` children; it cannot reference actual/frozen roots. Test setup creates the empty runs/scratch and three synthetic build files before qualification. Test manifests use the same closed schema/literals and canonical bytes but bind their synthetic M301 digest; the supplied manifest digest authenticates them. The shared reader checks this complete relation, with no skip-validation branch. Default manifest loading admits only the literal default digest/path above.

Export `M602SuccessorObserverIO` with exactly five mandatory trusted collaborators: `startApplication:typeof startLocalService`, `launchBrowser:typeof chromium.launch`, `runtimeRequest:OllamaNativeRequest`, `spawnGpu:typeof import('node:child_process').spawn`, and `connect:typeof import('node:net').connect`. Real defaults remain private; any supplied I/O object must contain all five before use. These seams inject existing primitive shapes, not new providers or arbitrary real roots. The observer owner exports `prepareM602SuccessorObservers(environment?, signal?:AbortSignal)`, where the optional complete environment is `{root:string,applicationRevision:string,io:M602SuccessorObserverIO}` and root must be the isolated UUID root. Absent environment uses the fixed production paths, prepared process revision and private primitives. Execution and qualification forward their caller signal unchanged; omission uses a private initially non-aborted signal. Already-aborted signals refuse all resource acquisition. Register cancellation before the first awaited stage, check before each subsequent stage, and retain cancellation/late-resource ownership throughout preparation and the returned session. Caller abort during an awaited stage closes admission immediately; a late resource is owned and closed without starting the next stage. Success returns `{ok:true,session}`; failure returns `{ok:false,error:'observer-readiness',cleanup:M602SuccessorCleanup}`. Session has `observers:M602Observers`, validated `gpuBefore`, `samples(timing):M602SuccessorSamples` and idempotent `close():Promise<M602SuccessorCleanup>`. Session methods freeze snapshots and never reopen work.

Tests live only in existing `tests/m602-generation.test.ts` (append successor integration using its public synthetic fixtures) and new `tests/m602-successor.test.ts` (observer/evidence/CLI contract and primitive lifecycle doubles). Existing accepted assertions remain unchanged. Code-owned files are the operation, CLI, two new successor helpers and exact manifest. Existing package, timing, historical, filesystem and production modules are reused unchanged. If a necessary cohesive owner falls outside these paths, stop for primary reconciliation instead of placing it in an unsuitable allowed module.

#### Closed records and qualification

All timestamps are canonical UTC ISO; hashes are lowercase SHA-256; counts/memory are finite safe nonnegative integers. Reject unknown fields at every level. Successor entered retains original fields and adds exactly `campaign:'m602-successor-v1'`, `campaignManifestSha256`, `qualificationSha256`; `manifestSha256` still denotes M301. Dispatch/result retain original fields and semantics with the new version. Readback requires current code/configuration, exact successor/M301 identities and all original source hash links.

`M602SuccessorQualification` is exactly:
`{version:'m602-successor-qualification-v1',campaign:'m602-successor-v1',qualifiedAt,campaignManifestSha256,manifestSha256,producerCodeSha256,applicationRevision,nodeVersion,browserVersion,build,ui:true,gpu:true,cleanup}`.
`applicationRevision` is the actual 40-hex HEAD supplied by the prepared command; default execution checks it against qualification. `nodeVersion` is `v24.20.0`, `browserVersion` is `151.0.7922.34`. `build` is the ordered three-entry array of `{path,sha256}`: `index.html`, `assets/index-C6L8S9Ht.css`, `assets/index-C7OtU_Ke.js`. Default bytes must match the accepted build inventory in M602-FUP-ACCEPT-01 at first qualification; each later read compares those ordinary files' current bytes with qualification. Isolated tests use the same paths with their synthetic contents. A different actual build inventory requires primary reconciliation before qualification, never an arbitrary client root.

`M602SuccessorCleanup` is exactly `{ui,runtime,gpu,application,browser,scratch}`, each `complete|'uncertain'|'not-created'`. A resource that was created cannot be called not-created. Qualification has complete UI/GPU/application/browser/scratch and runtime not-created (the in-memory runtime double creates no resource). Groq observation has all not-created. Local successful observation requires every field complete. A Local no-dispatch/early-terminal outcome may honestly record runtime not-created; it cannot qualify.

Qualification invokes the actual built service/browser with the same benign UI procedure and one actual baseline GPU sample, then exercises UI and synthetic runtime/GPU observer callbacks inside one 7000-ms synthetic window through the same coordinator. The already validated actual GPU sample supplies the synthetic GPU value; no second actual GPU command or runtime GET is made. This separates real primitive readiness from synthetic timing qualification. The signal, timer, parsers, state verification and cleanup path are the actual implementation; synthetic native/runtime evidence never masquerades as a model call or actual residency. It creates no case, dispatch, result or assessment. Require successful UI, actual GPU validation, completed synthetic observation slots and confirmed owned cleanup before exclusive qualification publication. Its `qualifiedAt` follows successful cleanup. Identity-preserving readback of qualification may reuse that procedure proof, but each case still provisions and verifies live resources anew.

#### Actual resources, admission and lifecycle

Default preparation requires the already-existing ordinary `data/runs`, the pinned built client, empty `temp/m104-ui`, and empty existing scanner/integration scratch; it does not create a run or alter developer data. Use `startLocalService({runRoot,clientRoot,applicationRevision,port:0})`, the same production options as main.ts. Do not invoke scan/retrieval/generation/review routes. Launch existing full managed Chromium with `channel:'chromium',headless:false,timeout:10000`; no browser acquisition or isolation-disabling argument. Create one fresh context with viewport 1280×800, acceptDownloads false and serviceWorkers block, one page, and no user profile. Admit browser requests only to the returned exact loopback origin using GET/HEAD; block and fail preparation/observation on any other request, popup, download, crash or page error. No external page navigation or form submission. Readiness verifies the built page, enabled Target URL and Local/Groq controls with Local selected.

Each service startup, browser launch, context/page setup and page-ready stage has a 10000-ms bound; individual UI actions have 5000-ms bounds. All preparation paths have a closing latch. Timeout/abort/failure closes admission before callbacks, prevents the next stage and retains ownership of any late-created service/browser/context/page or baseline GPU child. Observe and close late arrivals. Confirm closure within the separate 5000-ms cleanup deadline; pending resource acquisition at expiry is uncertain, not not-created. No readiness correction or later live operation proceeds until primary reconciles that uncertainty. Timeout races never erase ownership.

After verifying predecessors/qualification, provision actual Local app/browser and take one baseline GPU sample before exclusive case entry. Pre-entry preparation failure ends the command with no case/call. Corrected readiness may be prepared again only after confirmed prior cleanup; this cannot replay a consumed entry. After entry, retain unchanged metadata admission, durable dispatch marker and at most one native request. Do not manage, restart or unload the developer's Ollama process.

On native scheduling, each configured observer creates one 1000-ms timer; on expiry it attempts its gate once. Terminal/abort clears the timer. No polling, early retry, late replacement, extended generation window or held-open response is allowed. Delay is a fixed scheduling policy, not an optimum inferred from the previous 13.744-second call. A short call or cold model can fail the observation and stop the campaign.

The observer owner enforces `deadlineMs:5000` as one total elapsed-time deadline per collector, starting synchronously when the accepted `gate.start` invokes that collector's effect. The initial 1000-ms scheduling delay is excluded. The deadline covers all UI steps together, or the entire runtime request/body validation, or GPU spawn/output/validation; individual API or inactivity timeouts do not replace or restart it. Each collector has a private cancellation controller linked to the coordinator and caller signals. At expiry, latch the collector failed, discard its pending value, cancel/destroy its owned work and reject its effect promise; if native lifetime is still active this records a failed slot, while an earlier coordinator close retains its already-frozen aborted/missed status. No expiry changes or extends native lifetime. Subsequent UI steps and value commits are prohibited after this latch. Pass the linked collector signal to supported UI APIs and check it before every step. Late primitive settlement remains owned for cleanup and cannot turn rejection into success. Clear deadline timers/listeners when the effect settles or is cancelled. Baseline GPU uses the same total 5000-ms deadline from immediately before its one spawn invocation; failure prevents entry. Qualification uses these same collector deadline rules inside its separately bounded 7000-ms synthetic window. The separate cleanup deadline and resource-close proof remain mandatory even when the effect promise has already rejected.

UI effect checks abort immediately before every asynchronous step and passes the same signal where supported: focus Target URL, verify it is focused, change Local to Groq, verify Groq checked and Local unchecked. Record only all-true booleans; changing radio necessarily follows focus verification. Do not fill or submit a target. The UI mode is only this idle interface's state and does not change the case's immutable adapter.

Runtime effect performs exactly one GET to `http://127.0.0.1:11434/api/ps`, no redirect/retry/header credential. Require HTTP 200, application/json, complete bounded body at most 65536 bytes, strict UTF-8/JSON, one matching `qwen3.5:4b` record, exact accepted digest, context 32768, positive integer size and nonnegative size_vram no greater than size. Return only digest/context/size fields. Other model content is discarded. Own error listeners and explicitly destroy request/response on timeout/abort; timeout notification alone is insufficient. Confirm actual resource terminal/close, not just parsed response.

GPU sampling directly spawns `C:/Windows/System32/nvidia-smi.exe` with exactly `--query-gpu=memory.used,memory.free` and `--format=csv,noheader,nounits`, `shell:false`, `windowsHide:true`, bounded piped output (4096 bytes per stream), no inherited console or arbitrary arguments. Require exit 0 and one row with two nonnegative safe integers. Discard stderr content and emit no exception text. Abort/timeout requests termination of that exact owned child; require child close/stdio closure, not its killed flag. Baseline uses the same 5000-ms bound and records actual completion before entry; one during-call sample uses the coordinator signal. No numeric capacity threshold or OOM inference from memory totals is introduced.

Collectors check signal state before committing validated values. Coordinator timing freezes before abort notification; collectors freeze on abort and never accept a late mutation. Final samples include values only for completed timing slots; a subsequent resource close can improve cleanup evidence but cannot change timing/sample qualification. Exactly one coordinator owns native lifetime. All response/body/provider/credential validation and native request accounting remain unchanged.

After core settlement, close the owned page/context/browser and service, settle sampling resources, require successful service stop and confirm its loopback port closed; require assigned scratch empty and protected run data unchanged. Use one 5000-ms cleanup deadline for the complete cleanup group, continue owning late settlement and report uncertainty honestly. Never delete unknown scratch residue or use forced termination as proof of clean service/browser shutdown. Any failure exit, including a source-publication error, attempts this cleanup. No linked observation is invented when complete source hashes do not exist.

#### Observation, assessment and continuation

`M602SuccessorObservation` is exactly `{version:'m602-successor-observation-v1',campaign:'m602-successor-v1',caseLabel,campaignManifestSha256,qualificationSha256,producerCodeSha256,enteredSha256,dispatchSha256,resultSha256,diagnostic,timing,samples,cleanup}`. Reuse FUP's diagnostic and timing schemas/coherence/matrix unchanged; dispatch hash/window can be null consistently with result. Publish exclusively as the case's `observation.json` only after owned cleanup. Successful result publication does not excuse failed observation publication.

`M602SuccessorSamples` is null for Groq, otherwise exactly `{gpuBefore,ui,runtime,gpuDuring}`. Baseline is `{observedAt,usedMiB,freeMiB}`, completed before enteredAt. UI is `{textboxFocused:true,radioChanged:true,radioChecked:true}` or null. Runtime is `{digest,contextLength,sizeBytes,sizeVramBytes}` or null. During GPU is `{usedMiB,freeMiB}` or null. Each in-call value is non-null if and only if its corresponding timing slot is completed; validate values and require the full timing interval strictly within the authoritative native window. Failed/missed/aborted/unavailable slots have null values. Runtime/GPU assessment observedAt derives from the corresponding slot's finishedAt; no competing sample clock or late relabeling. Groq timing has unavailable slots and creates no Local resources.

`M602SuccessorAssessment` retains exactly the original assessment fields with successor evidence version and adds `observationSha256`. Bind it to complete source result and observation bytes; assessment time follows result and completed cleanup/publication. Local observation is the exact projection of linked timing/samples, plus the primary evaluator's boolean `oomObserved`; accepted requires false. A primary cannot manually supply different measurements to pass. Preserve the original ten dimension names/order, uncertainty and original scanner/retrieval limitations. No raw generated text enters the assessment.

Advance only when every earlier successor case has an intact current-code/current-manifest/current-qualification result, diagnostic integrity complete, qualified observation with confirmed cleanup, exact projection and accepted primary assessment. Generation failure, invalid/missing/unknown record, sample failure, OOM/unusable UI, drift, disclosure concern or uncertainty stops all subsequent dispatches. CLI execution exits nonzero for nonproposal or unqualified observation; zero means mechanically eligible for primary assessment, never semantic acceptance. Readback exit zero means valid records, including valid failed outcomes, not permission to continue. No original record, readback or assessment can pass a successor gate.

#### Commands, qualification and review gates

Use the maintained README preparation without acquisition. Original commands remain available for their original meanings. The parser adds exactly `execute-successor` and `readback-successor` modes with one case label, and a separate `{ok:true,mode:'qualify-successor-observers'}` success variant without caseLabel. Reject every duplicate, extra, alternate root/campaign or combined mode flag. Imports perform no effects. CLI projects only closed status/count/hash/observation/cleanup fields; never print a retained proposal or raw sample source.

All actual successor commands additionally set `A11Y_APPLICATION_REVISION` to current HEAD and restore its exact previous value in finally. Run through `Invoke-M105Command` with `$m105UiTemp` so browser paths/scratch and compile-cache environment are prepared/restored. Exact command payloads are:

```powershell

& $m105Node tests/helpers/m602-run-case.ts --qualify-successor-observers
& $m105Node tests/helpers/m602-run-case.ts --execute-successor --case local-image
& $m105Node tests/helpers/m602-run-case.ts --readback-successor --case local-image
```

For later cases substitute only the next literal in the frozen order after accepted predecessor assessment. Require explicit nonzero propagation. Readback may use the same wrapper without browser scratch because it starts no resource. Historical-only inspection remains unchanged. Before Groq, recheck current public documentation and privately established account limits; do not inspect credentials or issue a capability probe. Apply ordinary sequential spacing where current limits require it without changing controls or adding retries; byte admission does not prove hosted token fit. Account UI observations are not proof that the existing credential belongs to that project or that a request will succeed.

The focused offline command is the prior eight-file FUP command plus `tests/m602-successor.test.ts`, under maintained preparation, Node v24.20.0 and `--test --test-timeout=120000`, followed by independent strict TypeScript. Tests use complete primitive/native/credential doubles and exact isolated UUID scratch; no actual browser/GPU/runtime/provider/credential effects. Test preflight is read-only. Agreed absent first-module seam is `tests/helpers/m602-successor-observers.ts`; its new export may block initial Red only after independent existing runner readiness, with blocked assertions explicitly reported. Green must execute every unchanged assertion.

S1–S6 proof must cover wrong/cross-campaign identity, manifest/code/build/qualification drift, original predecessor rejection, occupancy/partial publication, every missing injected collaborator and no real fallback; true one-call count and stop/assessment gating; startup timeout and late resource arrival, already-aborted preparation and caller abort during every awaited preparation stage, execution/qualification/CLI preservation of cleanup receipts before entry and after partial publication, total collector deadline across multiple UI steps and continuously arriving HTTP bytes, deadline/native-close ordering and late settlement, pre-entry failure with cleanup, terminal before timer, equal timestamps, abort between each UI step, pending child/HTTP/context closure, malformed/oversized samples, late value/close, no Groq observer invocation, exact assessment projection and immutable readback. Reuse existing original/FUP negative tests for unchanged policy, not duplicate every predicate. A valid synthetic qualification must not depend on private frozen inputs or current dist files.

One coherent successor slice uses separately owned test preflight/Red and Green with the budgets in SUCCESSOR-01. Primary opens/closes exact leases and accepts actual diff, tests, strict and cohesion before fresh S3 review. Code corrections never renew live entries. After last executable change, run the maintained full suite with the new test, independent strict/build, exact-original historical inspection and preservation checks; rerun affected generated-asset consumers if build changes. Only after contract final review, accepted Green/S3 and these checks may primary create the literal root and invoke actual no-generation qualification. Then each of six operations remains individually gated; no batch or automatic continuation. Different final critical integration review audits actual outcomes or the stopped handoff, followed by documentation closure. M6-02 remains In progress until its complete Verification passes.

## M602-RESOLVE-01 — Bounded reasoning and evaluation reconciliation

Owner direction on 2026-09-22 authorizes resolving the audited M6-02 blockers and continuing the selected task. This is a new bounded correction scope; it renews none of the consumed historical campaign entries or worker chains. M6-02 remains In progress. M6-03/M6-04 remain unselected.

### Frozen Decision Review Contract

Status: initial synthesis DRAFT READY and pre-draft PASS; final review requests the bounded deadline correction recorded below. Target artifact: this section's execution decision and implementation packet, followed by a separately frozen six-case definition. The primary owns authority interpretation and writes. R3 applies because the change crosses complete-context admission, immutable configuration/provenance, cancellation, output parsing and historical-evidence integrity. Same model and runtime, existing corpus, fixed inputs, common proposal schema, rubric, privacy and human review remain controlling under REQ-LLM-005/008/011/019, REQ-EVAL-001/002/004–009, ADR-0003/0004/0005/0014/0023 and M6-02 Verification.

Candidate set, frozen before comparison:
A. Keep native schema-constrained thinking: two internal completions and a re-rendered reasoning prefix.
B. One native thinking completion: omit the Local format property, include the full issued case schema literally in the system message, validate the final JSON through the unchanged application contract.
C. Retain nonthinking behavior. This is the historical baseline; the owner's reasoning requirement is a hard gate.
An unsupported native reasoning-budget option, model/runtime replacement, truncation, output repair and automatic fallback are excluded.

Compare complete input preservation and pre-call fit, finite combined reasoning/final output, five-minute whole-operation limit, unchanged validation/grounding/privacy, recorded provenance, smallest coherent implementation, and honest failure reporting. Evidence classes: pinned runtime source, authenticated tokenizer proof, actual frozen request arithmetic, repository authorities and call graph, focused negative checks, then actual bounded capacity/response observations. No numeric score or model-quality ranking.

Decide now: exact schema placement, controls and configuration identity; whole-operation and transport deadline semantics; disposition of thinking text; fit formula and guard; historical readback; continuation versus case acceptance; scope of maintained evaluation adapter versus default application behavior. Prove later: exact three-case admission, parser/failure/cleanup behavior, useful valid answers, in-call reference-PC observations and actual Groq acceptance. Unknown runtime quality is not a deferred decision semantic.

Discovery establishes a possible B proof: for exactly one system and user message, input bound is their complete UTF-8 byte lengths plus 88 renderer bytes; schema and delimiter bytes are included in the system message. With 12288 combined generated tokens and a 32-position guard, require input + 12288 + 32 <= 32768. No second prompt is formed. Finite output and a 300000 ms deadline do not guarantee a final answer. The actual final prompt, all case enums and exact request bytes must be frozen and checked before dispatch.

Invariants:
- RES-I1 completeness: synthesis covers all candidates, controls, schema, deadlines, failure/continuation semantics and implementation responsibilities; analyst and both research reviewers.
- RES-I2 honesty: structural validity, semantic observations, transport completion, capacity and task completion remain distinct; no missing historical rejection reason is inferred; primary and final reviewer.
- RES-I3 authority: no failed Must requirement becomes passed, no model or runtime replacement, no later task or release qualification; primary and reviewers.
- RES-I4 fit/identity: exact final two messages and full schema survive serialization; absent format; finite combined reserve plus guard; exact runtime/model/tokenizer binding; oversize/mismatched requests dispatch zero times; test owner and critical implementation reviewer.
- RES-I5 privacy/cleanup: thinking is never logged or persisted; only final candidate goes to the unchanged validator; content-free closed diagnostic details; bounded one-call transport; uncertain cleanup stops dependent work; critical implementation reviewer.
- RES-I6 preservation: historical configurations, original inputs/corpus/rubric/results/assessments and consumed entries remain unchanged; any new campaign uses separate identity and original-output records; primary and final reviewer.

The canonical portfolio contract owns case acceptance. Reconciliation may separate permission to execute the next distinct case from semantic observation quality, but cannot silently change historical S6 or satisfy REQ-EVAL-008 with an invalid response. Any changed continuation policy must be explicit in the new frozen definition and readback. The six-case budget is not a retry pool.

Budgets: one bounded discovery report per assigned dimension/range; one comparative analyst synthesis plus at most one supported correction; one fresh pre-draft critical checkpoint plus at most one supported correction; primary draft; one different final critical research review plus at most one supported correction. No new generation during research. Re-enter research only for a named uncovered contradiction within those allowances; an exhausted barrier returns to primary for disposition, not an automatic reset. All readers synchronize at the primary. Freshness is the inspected tree and pinned sources on 2026-09-22; changed identities invalidate affected evidence.

After decision acceptance, use one coherent adapter slice and only a necessary campaign integration slice. Each receives preflight, separate test/code owners, initial and one ordinary correction per role, conditional third only under ADR-0024, exact path leases and one consolidated S3 review with one correction review. Focused checks during development; independent strict check/build and the authoritative suite once after integration. No new dependency or generalized experiment framework.

Live allowance under the owner's blocker-resolution direction: one separately identified representative full-stack capacity smoke for the changed Local configuration, then at most one generation per fixed case, six campaign calls. Seven calls maximum, with no retries. ADR-0004 requires capacity acceptance before entry into the fixed evaluation; the first campaign case cannot double as its prior capacity proof. A failed capacity smoke blocks all campaign entries. Freeze the smoke and six-case inputs/configuration before their respective calls; implementation verification, required review and actual observation readiness precede dispatch. Prior authorization to use the existing Ollama instance persists; no model download, replacement runtime, billing/account change, raw reasoning retention, commit or push is included. A failed/uncertain operation remains consumed; no replay. Actual changed-configuration capacity and provider results remain unproved.

Complete pre-edit coverage: three read-only discovery owners inspected contiguous decoded-character ranges [0,200000), [200000,400000), and [400000,594770) at SHA-256 b16d5f3b882a239cc69f16d7788e44719e006e51cd4c7c21818a6ff3b32a8ca4. ### M602-RESOLVE-DECISION-01 — Primary draft after the pre-draft checkpoint

The analyst returned DRAFT READY and the fresh pre-draft critical review passed with no findings. The supported end-to-end correction also returned DRAFT READY and passed its pre-draft checkpoint. The primary selects candidate B for the new ordinary Local default, separately frozen evaluation and prerequisite capacity smoke, subject to the different final correction review. The explicit legacy factory and historical configurations remain available unchanged. This resolves task-owned configuration literals under the accepted model/runtime/provider decisions; it establishes no capacity, quality or release qualification by itself.

The eight defined semantics in the reviewed synthesis are adopted here:
1. New prompt, adapter, effective-configuration and accounting identities; unchanged common proposal/case-schema meaning; historical tuples retain their original parameters and mixed tuples fail.
2. One system plus one user message. Common source-owned clarified instructions and only the selected rule's frozen blocking judgment. Local appends the entire issued schema with fixed measured delimiters and omits native format; Groq keeps its strict schema field. Controlled user bytes are unchanged. This prospectively supersedes historical native-schema placement and the prohibition on profile-specific instructions only for the new definition.
3. Local think:true, stream:false, context32768, combined output12288, temperature1/top_p0.95, truncate:false, shift:false, keep_alive5m, inherited top_k20/presence_penalty1.5 and the existing authenticated remaining defaults. One response, fixed model/endpoint; no stop strings, hidden reasoning-budget options, output repair or fallback.
4. Before dispatch, require complete detached system/user UTF-8 bytes +88 framing +12288 combined output +32 guard <=32768. Schema is counted once inside system. Reject malformed text, unsupported message shapes, identity mismatch or excess size before transport. Bind exact runtime/model/tokenizer/renderer/parser/effective settings. The reserve guarantees neither reasoning completion nor final-answer space.
5. Capture server operation-entry time before service admission, select the authenticated adapter profile's fixed duration and derive one absolute expiry: reasoning Local300000ms, legacy Local/Groq120000ms. Pass that expiry through generation stage, executor and transport; each uses remaining time, with metadata capped at10000ms. Standalone executor callers start their own outer budget. Recheck before dispatch, after asynchronous work/validation and immediately before admitting successful terminal publication. No inner stage restarts a full budget. Synchronous filesystem publication cannot be hard-preempted or guaranteed to finish at an exact wall-clock cutoff. Browser Local waits300000ms and Groq120000ms from user action using the same fixed browser-safe policy for its timer and elapsed check; it has an independent clock, so network delay may still yield the existing unknown outcome without replay. Preserve existing pending/unknown/settled states, ownership, announcements and markup. Cancellation/deadline latches failure and blocks late success. Retain bounded body and owned cleanup. Only the new parser permits a string message.thinking, discarding it before returning a candidate. Require expected model/assistant, complete HTTP, done:true/done_reason:stop and one whole JSON object; no prose extraction or length-stop acceptance. Never retain/log/expose hidden reasoning.
6. One separate genuine full-stack capacity smoke must pass before any fixed case. It uses real scan, embedding and supported retrieval through an explicit reasoning adapter, never the controlled-input exception. A mechanically validated proposal must reach usable pending review with no OOM/storage exhaustion/unusable UI, real in-call observations, intact identities and confirmed owned cleanup. Human semantic approval/save is unnecessary; defects remain separate observations. Invalid/failed/abstained smoke consumes its allowance and blocks the campaign.
7. Only the new campaign may advance after a recorded response-validation failure or negative semantic observation, and only with authenticated readback, intact identities, confirmed cleanup and acceptable UI/resource observations. Missing prerequisite, fit/identity failure, absent capacity, OOM/unusable UI or uncertain cleanup stops dependent calls. Every entered failure/uncertainty remains consumed. Invalid output fails its case and cannot satisfy REQ-EVAL-008 or task completion; a structurally valid unsupported answer retains its failed semantic observation and human edit/reject requirement. Earlier S6 and every historical result remain unchanged. This is distinct-case evaluation, never a retry.
8. Reuse cohesive generation, execution, transport and diagnostic owners. The reasoning request constructor accepts already-validated live GenerationInput; the ordinary Local resolver selects the new factory so the genuine smoke uses the real UI/service path. Preserve eligibility, the legacy factory/assembly/tuples and Groq default. A small browser-compatible policy owns fixed duration values; configuration authentication and operation ownership remain server-side. No arbitrary timeout setting, registry, test mode or special server facade is added. Production imports no evaluation helpers. The maintained runner's existing content-free diagnostic callbacks are reused; CAP11's discarded cause remains unknown.

Source proof anchors: [pinned chat handler](https://github.com/ollama/ollama/blob/v0.33.3/server/routes.go#L2574), [Qwen renderer](https://github.com/ollama/ollama/blob/v0.33.3/model/renderers/qwen35.go#L233), [completion options](https://github.com/ollama/ollama/blob/v0.33.3/llm/llama_server.go#L1441), [backend limits](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/blob/b10760/tools/server/server-context.cpp#L1724), [Qwen guidance](https://huggingface.co/Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B#best-practices), and the existing [M3-03 proof](m3-03-qwen-adapter-and-capacity-screen.md). These establish conditional mechanics, not quality or new capacity. The frozen synthesis is retained locally at temp/m602-resolve-v1/synthesis.md. RES-I1–RES-I6 remain cumulative.

The owner selects the proposed bounded correction before another model evaluation. Primary decision: create a distinct uncertainty profile while retaining old legacy/case/prompt/reasoning/judgment exports and exact wires. The new uncertainty leaf requires a string containing at least one non-whitespace character (minLength1 and pattern\\S), with a description and concise prompt instruction to name unresolved profile context in a sentence rather than a generic confidence label. Keep the existing nonblank runtime validator, confidence meanings and human-edit semantics; do not manufacture semantic certainty using fixed uncertainty text or an invented keyword classifier. A descriptive instruction and string constraint cannot guarantee factual support or meaningful reasoning. Models, parameters, context, output allowance and deadlines stay unchanged; the corrected profile becomes the ordinary Local/Groq default.

On2026-09-23 the primary checks https://console.groq.com/docs/structured-outputs: the selected gpt-oss-20b supports strict schemas and field descriptions, but the page does not explicitly enumerate minLength/pattern support. Hosted admission of these keywords remains unverified pending separately authorized actual evaluation. Do not substitute best-effort mode, retry, remove constraints silently or claim provider qualification from synthetic checks.

Add one opt-in bounded diagnostic channel alongside existing events. Local final-content parsing distinguishes JSON syntax failure from a successfully parsed non-object value. The existing frozen lexical policy identifies its matching rule and affected field. Preserve old rejection codes, old CandidateDetail shapes/readers, response validity, errors, cleanup and hidden-reasoning disposal. Emit only application-defined enums/field identifiers, never generated prose, JSON snippets, exception text, prompts, credentials or reasoning. Sink throws/rejections must not change generation results. No durable product field, telemetry subsystem or generic diagnostic framework is needed.

Responsibility placement: schema specialization stays in generation-case-request; instructions, version literals, configuration, fit, invocation validation and default resolution stay with existing owners. generation-diagnostics owns fixed detail types and safe sink emission; ollama-generation-http owns content parse classification; proposal-contract owns lexical validation/category selection. Existing factory/executor edges carry optional sinks only where needed. No new architectural layer, dependency, service or retry. Permit a focused local extraction only if it resolves actual cohesion and is reconciled before writing. The narrow new profile follows established tuple handling; no registry refactor.

### M602-UNCERTAINTY-DIAG — Offline validation diagnosis

Owner continuation selects repository-local diagnosis only after the completed uncertainty evaluation. R0 applies: deterministic inspection of retained records and existing code, with no external evidence or changed decision semantics. Primary performs one bounded in-memory probe; no new agent, application/test edit, provider/runtime operation, GPU work, credential access or generation occurs. Existing six-call allowances remain consumed.

Authentication confirms all 170 frozen producers and every saved result/observation/assessment against final-readback.json. Both failed cases received their exact frozen body. Their schema reference enums match the supplied fact identifiers for all three supported-text fields. The request assembler does not omit or rename a required reference. See [generation-case-request](../../../src/server/generation/generation-case-request.ts) and [Local wire construction](../../../src/server/generation/ollama-generation-fit.ts).

Nineteen synthetic in-memory checks use a detached copy of the already runtime-valid contrast proposal and the authenticated Local contrast validation context. A neutral rescan/human-verification reminder passes. An affirmative certification sentence, its negation and a neutral reminder followed by a compliance disclaimer each fail at postChangeVerificationReminder/prohibited-claim with the same certification-conformance-compliance category. All eight exact evidence identifiers pass individually. Five distinct deviations (case, trailing whitespace, shortened name, guidance ID in the evidence array and a number) each reproduce userImpact.evidenceReferences/reference-value. A duplicate produces the different reference-duplicate code; an empty userImpact evidence array passes its existing zero-minimum rule. These controlled mutations demonstrate validator boundaries, not the content of the two actual failed responses. No saved proposal is edited.

Confirmed interpretation: the label response reached the final generic candidate field and matched the first lexical category. The frozen m301-prohibited-v1 policy explicitly includes negation; it is not a semantic classifier. No retained evidence distinguishes an affirmative prohibited assertion from a harmless disclaimer, and no new bug record is justified for behavior already specified by the frozen policy. Contrast reached the userImpact reference-item membership check. It does not establish an empty array, duplicate, precise misspelling, or validity of fields checked afterward. The exact invalid item and reminder are unavailable because rejected candidates are intentionally discarded. No additional offline replay can recover them.

The existing prompt already requests exact unique reference strings and avoids certif/conform/complian stems. The reminder schema remains type:string. In Local reasoning mode the complete schema is prompt text and the wire omits format; therefore its enums guide the model but do not constrain decoding. This explains how an invalid reference can reach application validation, not why the particular token was selected. It is intentional under M602-RESOLVE-DECISION-01, rather than an accidentally dropped format option. Groq retains its separate strict-schema path. Both Local failures are completed response-validation outcomes, not timeout, context-fit, transport, runtime or memory failures; increasing the deadline is not a demonstrated correction.

Next correction candidate: request one fixed neutral post-change reminder, such as requiring a rescan and relevant human verification without certification vocabulary. This can also address the generic Groq label reminder, but any shared prompt/schema change creates new identities and affected-case obligations. An exact reminder does not solve contrast references. Before another prompt-only campaign, examine a bounded way to enforce final-answer schema while retaining required reasoning and complete-input fit. Do not silently restore native format: the prior decision rejected its second internal completion/re-rendered prompt under the existing fit and resource assumptions. No alternative is selected or implemented here; that material execution change needs its own scoped decision and verification. Keep exact-reference rejection, source-support requirements, original outputs and no-fallback behavior intact. Do not normalize invented references, assign citations automatically, relax the frozen lexical policy or claim these synthetic checks qualify model output.

Roadmap status, public capability statements, progress index, requirements and ADRs remain accurate and unchanged. M6-02 remains In progress; no task completion, new live allowance or implementation authorization is inferred from diagnosis.

### M602-SCHEMA-FEASIBILITY — Native schema and reasoning accounting

Owner continuation selects offline feasibility investigation of final-answer schema enforcement while preserving reasoning and a fixed neutral reminder. Research identity M602-SCHEMA-FEASIBILITY-20260923 uses answer-only R3 because second-pass serialization, context integrity and reasoning privacy fall outside the frozen single-completion proof. One critical researcher supplies the pinned-runtime evidence; primary synthesizes it with repository arithmetic; a fresh critical research reviewer returns PASS without findings. The coordinating capsule allows one targeted researcher follow-up and one bounded answer correction; neither is used. No comparative execution decision, analyst-triggering contradiction, implementation lease or live operation is introduced. M602-RESOLVE-DECISION-01, RES-I1–RES-I6 and REQ-LLM-005/008/011/019 plus REQ-EVAL-004/005/008 remain controlling.

Pinned Ollama v0.33.3 supports think:true together with JSON-schema format. The rendered-chat handler conditionally cancels the first completion after accumulated thinking reaches parsed final content, appends the thinking as an assistant prefix, and starts one constrained second completion. Both receive the same options; first-pass usage is aggregated rather than subtracted from the second allowance. Consequently num_predict12288 permits at most24576 generated tokens across the two completions. This is a bound, not guaranteed two-pass execution, actual overflow or doubled duration. Both passes inherit the request context, but successful completion and native cancellation cleanup remain untested. Sources: [chat handler](https://github.com/ollama/ollama/blob/v0.33.3/server/routes.go#L2587), [completion options](https://github.com/ollama/ollama/blob/v0.33.3/llm/llama_server.go#L1441).

For the retained two-message shape, the [Qwen35 renderer](https://github.com/ollama/ollama/blob/v0.33.3/model/renderers/qwen35.go#L264) contributes88 initial framing bytes and99 after appending trimmed thinking. If B1 is the existing complete UTF-8 input bound and T is accumulated thinking, the conservative second-prompt bound is B1+11+UTF8(T). Reusing the32-position guard and full second reserve requires B1+11+max UTF8(T)+12288+32 <=32768. Current image/label/contrast B1 values16227/18332/18710 leave4210/2105/1727 reasoning bytes under that conservative formula, before the reminder delta. These are proof headrooms, not measured token consumption or evidence of actual overflow. The existing theorem bounds tokens from known bytes; it does not bound decoded/re-tokenized reasoning from a generated-token cap. An authenticated worst-case bound, including parser and boundary effects, plus exact revised request arithmetic is still required. The [version-linked backend](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/blob/b10760/tools/server/server-context.cpp#L1724) can reject oversized prompts or stop at context capacity with shifting disabled; post-dispatch failure is not a pre-call fit proof.

Primary authenticates all170 producer hashes and six saved wire hashes, independently rechecked by the reviewer. The candidate reminder, "After changes, rescan and perform relevant human verification.", has62 UTF-16 units. Replacing its type:string schema with a singleton enum adds74 serialized JSON UTF-8 bytes. Substitution into detached copies of all four runtime-valid proposals passes existing generic candidate validation. No saved response or manifest is edited. These checks establish wording compatibility and arithmetic only: a Local prompt enum remains advisory, and neither exact-schema model behavior nor semantic source support is established.

The [runtime parser](https://github.com/ollama/ollama/blob/v0.33.3/model/parsers/qwen35.go#L91) can expose reasoning at trace logging level; this is also present on the current thinking path and is not evidence of application persistence. Preserve the application discard boundary and assess runtime logging separately in any future execution design. Schema-subset behavior, transition/completion, quality, timing, capacity and native cleanup remain unproved. Do not enable native format, increase time, repair rejected citations or repeat the campaign from this research result. The next engineering prerequisite is complete two-pass admission accounting; any selected change needs its own scoped decision, identities and affected verification before separately authorized live evaluation.

Documentation impact: primary records this accepted research checkpoint in the living plan only. Requirements, roadmap status, public capability statements, progress summary, executable configuration and consumed call allowances remain unchanged. No model/provider/runtime/GPU operation, application/test edit or full-suite repetition occurs. Proportional link/consistency checks and git diff --check close this checkpoint; M6-02 remains In progress.

### M602-SCHEMA-FOLLOWUP — Retokenization counterexample

The owner directs continuation of the second-pass accounting work before conditional implementation. This uses the existing M602-SCHEMA-FEASIBILITY-20260923 targeted follow-up, not another model evaluation or renewed historical allowance. The critical researcher returns RESEARCH COMPLETE; a fresh critical research reviewer independently reproduces the decisive evidence and returns PASS with no findings. Primary accepts SF07–09, retaining SF01–06 and RES-I1–RES-I6. The follow-up allowance is now consumed. No different execution configuration or contract is selected.

Primary and reviewer separately read only the installed model's bounded GGUF metadata region: 11,935,649 bytes, SHA-256 67acb20727d6c482755f0bc14c5914a48a249a5e9ce4be72e18f2300383f6588, matching [the retained artifact proof](m3-03-qwen-adapter-and-capacity-screen.md#m303-g-actual-01--completed-accounting-evidence-for-synthesis). It contains 52 keys, 248,320 vocabulary tokens and 247,587 merges. No tensors, runtime, inference or GPU are used. Pure ASCII BPE calculation finds normal token242875 (accro) and39821 (ubic), each individually canonical, concatenate to accroubic and re-tokenize into4327 (acc),566 (rou),90277 (bic). Ordered merge ranks are o+u26, i+c35, a+c309, r+ou310, ac+c4071, b+ic90021; neither remaining adjacent pair has a merge. The bounded search stops at this witness; the reviewer reproduces that exact witness without broader search.

The [pinned BPE decoder and tokenizer](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/blob/b10760/src/llama-vocab.cpp#L576) rebuild tokens from text without retaining sampled boundaries. The [Qwen35 pretokenizer](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/blob/b10760/src/unicode.cpp#L622) splits numeric codepoints separately. Therefore repeating the vocabulary sequence accro, ubic, 0 yields a source-derived three-to-four token mapping per unit: 4,095 units use12,285 initial tokens and16,380 re-tokenized tokens; adding12,288 final reserve and32 guard leaves4,068 of32,768 positions for the rest of the prompt. This is a tokenizer construction, not observed model reasoning. Sampling reachability under the fixed controls, a successful thinking-to-content transition and overflow for any actual case remain unknown.

The rejected proof strategy is universal token-count nonexpansion. The existing known-byte upper bound remains valid. Primary also finds a128-byte maximum normal vocabulary token; the resulting loose byte allowance does not establish admission at current settings and is not adopted as a complete parser/Unicode bound. The missing evidence remains an authenticated uniform bound for the complete second rendered prompt, including decode, parser, Unicode and boundary effects, that satisfies secondPromptBound+12288+32 <=32768 for every admitted request. Post-dispatch runtime rejection does not satisfy [REQ-LLM-008](../../requirements/generation-provider-and-model-lifecycle/GENERATION_PROVIDER_EXECUTION.md#llm-provider-selection-and-generation-execution). The finding establishes neither impossibility nor permission to relax that requirement.

Disposition: do not enable native format or independently ship the reminder as a claimed solution to both failures. A different bounded accounting mechanism or execution/control contract needs an explicit decision before dependent implementation; the current proof attempt cannot be repeated under a new identifier. M6-02 remains In progress. No application/test edit, live request, setting change, billing action, retained-response modification or full-suite repetition occurs. Documentation impact: this plan alone records the accepted finding, its limits and next decision boundary; navigation, status consistency and git diff --check receive proportional verification.

R3 applies to changed admission semantics, custom request/configuration identity and native reasoning serialization/privacy. ### M602-NATIVE-CONTRACT — Frozen implementation semantics for review

Create Local-only identities m602-native-schema-instructions-v1, m602-native-schema-v1, m602-ollama-native-schema-v1, m602-qwen35-native-schema-32768-v1 and m602-qwen35-initial-prompt-bound-v1. The system contains the existing uncertainty instructions, an exact neutral reminder instruction, and the complete case schema with the existing measured delimiters. Add native format with that same schema. The fresh schema adds a singleton enum for postChangeVerificationReminder: "After changes, rescan and perform relevant human verification." Keep existing exact reference/judgment enums and the unchanged generic validation contract. Native schema limitations, including unsupported pattern/uniqueness behavior, never relax the application's nonblank, reference, prohibited-claim or semantic-review checks. A mismatch fails response validation without output repair.

Native controls are think:true, stream:false, truncate:false, shift:false, keep_alive5m, num_ctx32768, num_predict12288, temperature1 and top_p0.95, with existing authenticated inherited defaults. One outer HTTP generation dispatch may contain at most two native completions, each capped at12288; the aggregate upper bound is24576 generated tokens, not a guaranteed allocation or completed answer. No per-pass deadline reset or new application request is introduced. Existing non-stop, malformed, excess-body, timeout, HTTP rejection and uncertain-cleanup handling remains failure, with actual attempted provenance once dispatch begins. Client HTTP cleanup never claims native runner cancellation.

Before native dispatch, count the complete detached rendered first prompt using UTF8(systemIncludingSchema)+UTF8(user)+88. Require initialInputTokens+12288+32 <=32768. Do not count the separately transmitted grammar as another prompt copy; it is the same schema already included in system text. Reject unsupported message shapes, malformed Unicode, tuple/schema identity mismatch, excess initial bound or altered controls before generation. Introduce a distinct initial-prompt-upper-bound accounting/fit variant so downstream code cannot mistake this check for complete two-pass fit. Its fixed configuration binds context32768, per-completion output12288, maximum native completions2 and maximum aggregate generated tokens24576; the fit reports the initial bound and first-pass reserve. Explicitly do not claim advance fit for the runtime's reasoning-prefixed second prompt. Runtime context rejection or exhaustion is an attempted bounded failure, never permission to truncate evidence or publish a partial proposal.

Primary reconciles REQ-LLM-008, ADR-0020, the forward evaluation amendment, BHV-03, SPEC-003 and HS-008 for this prospective distinction. The future Local cases require new identities and affected evidence; unchanged Groq observations may be reused only after exact compatibility is established. ### M602-NATIVE-A — Cohesive offline implementation slice

Workflow M602-NATIVE-20260923; task M6-02; slice M602-NATIVE-A; standard nonvisual profile; TDD Applicable. Separate persistent test_worker and code_worker own sequential preflight, Red and Green under exact leases. Primary owns authorities, plan, baselines and evidence acceptance. S3 review covers custom identity/admission and reasoning/privacy/failure boundaries after integration.

Production responsibility placement: generation-artifacts.ts owns new stable identities and the fixed reminder literal; reasoning-generation-configuration.ts owns the immutable Local profile and deadline selection; reasoning-generation-instructions.ts owns its model-visible instruction extension; generation-case-request.ts specializes and issues the profile schema; generation-input.ts selects the profile instructions from real admitted evidence; generation-contract.ts owns honest accounting/fit and invocation types/readers; generation-fit.ts validates the exact profile and initial-only fit; ollama-generation-fit.ts owns detached wire serialization and arithmetic; ollama-generation.ts exposes the new factory through the existing transport; proposal-contract.ts owns a narrow exact-reminder validation helper without changing generic durable validation; generation-execution.ts applies that fresh-profile helper alongside existing profile-judgment checks before/after candidate normalization; local-service/generation-adapters.ts selects the new Local default. Existing HTTP transport is reused unchanged unless preflight establishes a concrete contracted gap requiring primary scope reconciliation. No new dependency, registry, tokenizer, orchestration framework or user configuration is added.
